## Document 017: 2018 年の過去問は使えるか 利上げ打ち止めが株価反転の起点に

Source PDF: all report pdf/2018 年の過去問は使えるか 利上げ打ち止めが株価反転の起点に.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-24
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

2026年 11月 24日(火)

1/4 
2018年の過去問は使えるか 利上げ打ち止めが株価反転の起点に 
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・10月中旬以降のＡＩ・半導体株の下落要因は、ＡＩ投資の減速懸念もさることながら、金利
上昇が効いていると筆者は判断している。３月以降の原油高を受けて、主要中央銀行が利上
げに踏み切り、世界的にイールドカーブ全体が上昇したことで、①株式の債券に対する相対
的な魅力が低下したほか、②金利負担増加がＡＩ関連投資を抑制するとの懸念が生じた。②
は米新興ＡＩ企業の資金調達を巡る懸念にも繋がった。その他、③米中間選挙における民主
党の躍進により生じたＡＩ投資規制の強化懸念、④エヌビディアＣＥＯの辞任（その後復
帰）など特殊な要因も重なった。

・このうち④は事態が急変した。エヌビディア社は11月23日にジェンセン・ファン氏の即時復
帰を発表。これによって、当面、ＡＩ産業における同氏の属人的問題は解消されたと考えて
問題ないだろう。とはいえ、①、②、③に絡んだ問題は残存している。

・このうち③の民主党によるデータセンター投資の規制強化に対する観測は、新体制で議会が
発足する2027年１月以降も蒸し返される可能性があるだろう。全体としてみれば、民主党は
必ずしもデータセンター建設、すなわちＡＩ開発に否定的ではないものの、選挙の焦点の一
つであったデータセンターの建設許可は政治的な人質になり易く、直ちに解決するとは考え
・日経平均株価は先行き 12ヶ月 72,000円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPYは先行き 12ヶ月 155円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を 12月に 1.5％とした後、27年末までに 2.25％とするだろう 
・FEDはFF金利（誘導目標上限）を、12月に4.25％へ引き上げるだろう

### Page 2

2/4 
にくい。データセンターの新設が後ずれするだけでも、ネオクラウドなど新興ＡＩ各社の資
金繰り懸念は高まってしまう。財務体質の強いハイパースケーラーにとってもＡＩ投資のキ
ャッシュフロー好転や収益化の遅れは大きな痛手となる。

・他方、①と②はここへ来て最悪期脱出の兆候もみられるようになってきた。最も重要なのは
原油価格の低下であろう。ＷＴＩ原油価格は11月23日に80ドルを割れる場面があった。Ａ
Ｉ・半導体株の逆風となっている世界的な金利上昇について、その背景に原油高があること
は論を俟たない。ここで夏場以降、米実質金利上昇と米国株ＰＥＲ低下が併存してきたこと
に改めて注目したい。ＡＩ・半導体株を巡っては「業績見通しが上向いているのに株価がつ
いてこない、それどころか下落する」と言われてきた。このグラフは、金利上昇がこの疑問
解消にあたって非常に重要な要因であることを如実に物語る。夏場以降、「一時的」とみら
れていた原油高が長期化したことで、Ｆｅｄを中心とする主要中央銀行の利上げ観測が高ま
ることで金利が上昇し、その裏で株式のバリュエーションが低下していったことがわかる。

・Ｆｅｄが一段とタカ派に傾斜するようだと、株式市場は更なる調整に見舞われることにな
る。振り返ると、10月ＣＰＩが発表される直前の11月10日にウォーラー理事が突如として
50bpの利上げに言及したことが、その後の株価急落のきっかけとなったように思える。同時
期にエヌビディアＣＥＯの進退問題が大きく取り沙汰されたことで、ウォーラー理事の発言
はかき消された感があったが、この発言によって大幅利上げに対する警戒感が強まり、株式
市場への打撃となったことは認識しておく必要があろう。他方、ウォーラー理事と同等の影
響力を持つウィリアムズ・ＮＹ連銀総裁は追加利上げの必要性を認めつつも「関税、地政学

### Page 3

3/4 
リスク、ＡＩ投資による値上がりは一部の財に表れているものの、幅広く持続的なインフレ
へ大きく波及している証拠は乏しい」との見解を示しており、現時点で大幅な利上げに距離
を置いている。この点は株式市場にとって安心感がある。同総裁は「来年に２％をやや上回
る水準まで鈍化した後、2028年にＦＲＢが目標とする２％に達する」との予想を示してい
る。

・これらを踏まえると相場の潮目が変化するのは、現時点でタカ派方向へ傾倒しているウォー
ラー理事の見解がハト派方向に変化する際ではないか。もちろん、それはインフレデータ次
第であるが、足もとで原油価格が低下していることに鑑みると、同理事のインフレ警戒感は
今後徐々に弱まっていっても不思議ではない。またＡＩ・半導体株の急落によって金融環境
が急速かつ過度に引き締まってしまうことを懸念する可能性もある。

・バーナンキ氏の後に続くＦＲＢ議長、すなわちイエレン氏やパウエル氏は株式市場の急落に
際してハト派的な見解を示し、相場反転のきっかけとなり、それは「イエレン・プット」な
どと言われた。そうした市場への口先介入に距離を置くウォーシュ現議長は「ウォーシュ・
プット」という言葉をいかにも嫌いそうであり、株価急落局面でも口数の少ない役を演じ切
りそうである。そうした事情もあり、ウォーラー理事やウィリアムズ総裁の発言が存在感を
増しそうである。

・なお、過去数ヶ月の株価下落は2018年との類似性が指摘される。当時も現在も、景気が底堅
く推移するもとで、Ｆｅｄが利上げを継続して実質金利が上昇し、株価がＰＥＲ低下を伴っ
て下落した。2018年は株価下落にもかかわらず、Ｆｅｄが12月も利上げを継続し、なおかつ
（今よりずっと信頼されていた）ドットチャートが翌年の追加利上げを示唆していた。株式
相場の転換点となったのは、2019年１月４日のパウエル議長の発言。株式市場をなだめる意
図もあってか「政策判断を忍耐強く行い、市場の示すリスクに注意を払い、必要なら政策姿
勢を大きく変更する」と軌道修正を図った。結局、株価は2018年12月24日が大底となり、そ
の後はＦｅｄの利下げ（2019年7・9・10月）を追い風として、コロナ禍に直面するまで
S&P500は10％超の下落を経験することなく上昇した。

・2018年の過去問を今次局面に応用すると、12月ＦＯＭＣにおける利上げが最後になるとの見
方が強まれば、株価はその近辺が底になるのではないか。そうした意味においても上記２名

### Page 4

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 歐洲企業協會(Business Europe)發布一項調查顯示，自 6月以來，歐洲企業對工
業和服務業前景的信心有所改善，惟對通膨上升和主權債務問題的擔憂日益加
劇。由於美伊戰爭推高能源成本，而歐盟絕大部分能源依賴進口，能源價格上漲
進一步傳導至零售價格，導致今年通膨升至歐洲央行 2%目標水準之上，市場預
期歐洲央行將於 12月再度升息。
 美國舊金山聯準銀行總裁戴利(Mary Daly)表示，價格壓力正從能源和商品擴散
至服務業，而服務業通膨可能非常頑固。潛在通膨可能並未像市場預期的降
溫。近期能源價格回落，拉低整體通膨，然而核心通膨仍持續往上，聯準會需
要進一步升息以應對居高不下的通膨。此外，聯準會理事克里斯沃勒
(Christopher Waller)及達拉斯聯準銀行總裁洛根(Lorie Logan)均提出升息兩碼的
可能性，多位聯準會官員頻頻釋放偏鷹言論，升息預期升溫，預計將支撐美元

走勢。
 日本 10 月企業商品物價指數年增率由前月的 6.8%上升至 7.1%，主因為中東衝
突及 AI相關需求推升能源和晶片價格，通膨持續上行，提供日本升息有利條件，
有利日幣走升。
 美國 10月 CPI月增率 0.4%、核心 CPI月增率 0.3%雙雙高於預期，10月 PPI年
增率亦升至 6.5%，為四年來新高，通膨居高不下，FedWatch 顯示 12 月升息機
率達九成，2027年 1 月再升息一碼機率逾六成。
 台灣 10月出口 967.6 億美元，為史上單月首度突破 900億美元，年增率 58.9%
為連續 36個月正成長，創下史上最長成長周期。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Nov/16/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.794，低點在 32.994，以 32.838 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 7 分。 
    上週台幣走貶，週初，台股反彈，台幣一度走升至 32.794週內高點，不過，外
資延續匯出走勢，台幣隨即收斂所有漲幅，並貶回 32.950 上方；週間，美國公布 10
月 CPI數據表現優於預期，數據強化 12月升息一碼的預期，同時也開始反應升息兩
碼的風險，美元指數強勢突破 103 整數關卡，美元利差優勢擴大，台幣承壓走低至
32.994週內低點；臨近週末，台股走揚，外資轉為買超台股並匯入，台幣升破 32.830，
隨後匯價逢低吸引進口商買盤進場，終場在央行調節之下收在 32.838。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。就國際市場而言，美國 10月 CPI優於市場預期，顯
示通膨壓力仍在，市場預期聯準會 12月升息一碼，也不排除一口氣升息兩碼，美元
利差優勢將不利台幣表現；就台灣市場來看，上週公佈台灣 10月出口 967.6億美元，
是史上單月首度突破 900 億美元，寫下單月新高，且年增率 58.9%創下連續 36紅的
表現。基本面強勁，將有助於台幣的表現；盤勢上來看，輝達股價下跌風波，連帶
影響台股表現，整體外資仍偏向匯出，台幣仍面臨貶值的壓力。綜合以上，預估本
週台幣走勢偏弱。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.600-33.200

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Nov/16/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣區間震盪。週初，川普表示若伊朗願意簽署具有實質內容的協議，
美方就不太可能重新展開大規模的攻擊，帶動市場進一步降低戰事升溫的預期，油
價下跌，風險偏好回升，減弱美元避險需求，人民幣受益走升至 6.6650；週間，聯
準會官員沃勒鷹派發言，加以美國 10月 CPI表現優於預期，帶動美債殖利率及美元
指數走揚人民幣走貶至 6.6800；臨近週末，美國 10 月 PPI 年增率 6.5%，創下 2022
年 11 月以來高點，顯示通膨壓力難以消退，美元指數高檔震盪，人民幣於 6.6700上
方走勢偏弱，終場境外人民幣收在 6.6746，週線貶值 10點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6726，
週線升值 37點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣區間震盪。就國際市場來看，美國 10月 CPI優於市場預期，
強化市場對於聯準會 12月升息的預期，利差因素支撐美元指數反彈，將抑制人民幣
走升；就中國國內市場來看，中國最新公布 10 月貿易順差規模持續超過 1000 億美
元，出口商結匯需求將緩解人民幣貶值壓力；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖
中，USD/CNH重返五日均線之上，顯示人民幣強勢升值的趨勢暫歇，綜合以上，預
估本週人民幣區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6600-6.6900

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Nov/16/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週日圓走弱。週初，USD/JPY 在 158 整數關卡附近波動；週間，美國 10 月
CPI數據表現優於預期，且多位聯準會官員發表鷹派言論，帶動美元指數回升至 102
以上，USD/JPY 上行突破 160 整數關卡；臨近週末，美國 10 月 PPI 年增率 6.5%為
四年來新高，USD/JPY持穩於高位，終場收在 159.66，週線上漲 121 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本公布 10 月企業商品物
價指數年增率由前月的 6.8%上升至 7.1%，主因為中東衝突及 AI相關需求推升能源
和晶片價格，通膨持續上行，提供日本升息有利條件，預計將支撐日幣走勢。二、
美國 10月 CPI月增率 0.4%、核心 CPI月增率 0.3%皆高於預期，10 月 PPI 亦升至高
位，加之多位聯準會官員鷹派發言，FEDWATCH顯示 12月升息機率已上升至 90%，
明年 1 月再升息一碼機率也超過 60%，美元持穩於 102以上高位，將有利 USD/JPY
走勢。三、日本財務大臣片山皋月表示近期匯市走勢出現單邊快速波動，日本政府
將繼續與美國財政部密切合作，並採取適當應對措施，日本政府逢高採取干預將限
縮 USD/JPY上行空間。四、就技術面而言，在日線圖中，USD/JPY突破七周來的主
要交易區間以及年線壓力，在 160 整數關口徘徊，短線多方動能較佔優勢，然而匯
價來至高點有日本政府干預風險，預期將限縮上行幅度。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY
本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：158.50-161.00 
前週交易區間

美元兌歐元匯率走勢

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 震盪走跌。週初，市場對於美伊衝突的擔憂有所緩解，風險偏
好回升，國際油價及美元指數同步下行，EUR/USD 自上周低點 1.1135 附近走升最
高來至 1.1250；週間，美國公布 10月份 CPI顯示通膨仍處高位且多位聯準會官員鷹
派談話，EUR/USD 下行跌破 1.11 整數關卡；臨近週末，美國 10 月份 PPI 年增率
6.5%為四年來新高，EUR/USD續跌，終場收在 1.1021，週線下跌 122 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、德國 10 月 CPI 月減 0.2%
年增 2.8%，與預期相符且較 9 月下滑，顯示通膨有所放緩；此外德國央行總裁暨歐
洲央行管委納格爾表示德國經濟可能在第四季加速，儘管能源價格在美國期中選舉
後下跌，但因通膨仍高於 2%的目標，下個月升息依然是顯而易見，歐洲央行維持鷹
派態度將帶給歐元支撐。二、美國 10月 CPI及 PPI 顯示通膨依然頑固且有向更廣泛
經濟領域擴散的趨勢，此外多位聯準會官員發表談話，其中理事沃勒及達拉斯聯儲
總裁洛根均提出升息 2 碼的可能性，升息預期增加將持續支撐美元。三、技術面而
言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 各天期均線向下發散，近期匯價接連跌破 1.12 及 1.11
整數關卡，短線空方動能強勁，短線上以震盪偏弱看待。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD
本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，本週初央行積極減發定存單，令市場資金充裕，雖台股連

低位。

場預期區間 1.56%-1.57%，較上次標售結果下跌 3.8 個基本點。

1.67%~1.68%，利率高位較上週下跌 1個基本點。 
二、 本週台債殖利率先下後上，週初先落後反應上週美債利率下跌走勢，5 年券
A15108、10年券 A15109分別向下成交在 1.71%、1.86%，後因美債利率反彈，
10 年券 A15109R週五成交在 1.93%。 
受美國 CPI 高於預期、多位官員表達偏鷹派言論、川普表示無須與伊朗達成
協議影響，美債利率回升，加上台灣央行年底升息可能性尚未排除，買盤缺乏
追價動力，預期短線台債利率呈區間盤整，預估 5 年券區間為 1.80%~1.85%，
10 年券區間為 1.90%~2.00%。

## Document 077: 2026年7-9月期の実質GDP～前期比▲0.1％（年率▲0.3％）を予測～

実質ＧＤＰ成長率の推移
民間最終消費支出 民間企業設備 その他民需 公需 外需
（資料）内閣府経済社会総合研究所「四半期別ＧＤＰ速報」
( )内は年率換算値
(注）2602までは当研究所による改定見込値、2603は予測値
予測

１. 11/16に内閣府から公表される 2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDPは、前期比▲0.1％（前期比年
率▲0.3％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成長になったと推計される。 
２. 高水準の企業収益を背景に設備投資が前期比 1.4％と大幅に増加したが、天候不順の影響
などから民間消費が前期比▲0.3％と減少したこと、原油を中心に輸入が大幅に増加し、
外需が成長率を大きく押し下げたことなどから、小幅ながら 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成
長になったとみられる。 
３. 名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率 2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質 GDPの伸
びを大きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％、前年比 2.5％と予測する。 
４. 2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時
的な影響が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回
復を妨げる可能性が高い。 
５. 現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むこ
となどから、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまる
と予想している。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-10-30

2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP 
～前期比▲0.1％（年率▲0.3％）を予測～

経済研究部 経済調査部長 斎藤  太郎 
(03)3512-1836  tsaito@nli-research.co.jp

### Page 2

名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率 2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質 GDPの伸びを大
きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％（4-6月期：同 1.0％）、前年比 2.5％（4-6月
期：同 2.6％）と予測する。

なお、11/16に内閣府から 2026年 7-9月期の GDP速報が発表される際には、基礎統計の改定や季
節調整のかけ直しなどから、成長率が過去に遡って改定される。当研究所では、2026年 4-6月期の
実質 GDP成長率は、外需、設備投資が上方修正されることから、前期比年率 1.4％から同 2.3％へ
上方修正されると予想している。

2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時的な影響
が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回復を妨げる可能性が
高い。消費者物価上昇率は 2026年 9月に 9ヵ月ぶりの 2％台となったが、原材料価格の上昇を耐久
財や日用品などの販売価格に転嫁する動きが今後さらに広がることから、2026年度末にかけて 3％
台まで高まる公算が大きい。物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下を背景に 2026年度後半
の民間消費は弱めの動きが続くことが予想される。 
現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むことなどか
ら、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまると予想している。

1 10/30までに公表された経済指標をもとに予測している。今後公表される経済指標の結果によって予測値を修正する可
能性がある。

### Page 3

民間消費は前期比▲0.3％と予測する。 
実質賃金はプラスの伸びが続いているが、物価上昇率の高まりによって実質購買力が押し下げら
れていることに加え、台風や豪雨、長雨などの天候不順が個人消費を下押しした。 
2026年 7-9月期の消費関連指標を確認すると、延べ宿泊者数（うち日本人）は前期比 2.8％（4-6
月期：同▲0.9％）の増加となったが、自動車税の
「環境性能割」の廃止を受けて 4-6月期に前期比
10.7％の高い伸びとなった自動車販売台数が、その
反動で同▲7.5％と落ち込んだほか、外食産業売上
高が前期比▲0.3％（4-6月期：同 0.2％）、小売業
販売額指数が前期比▲2.1％（4-6月期：同 0.6％）
と減少した（いずれもニッセイ基礎研究所による
季節調整値、外食産業売上高、小売業販売額指数
は消費者物価指数で実質化）。

・住宅投資～弱めの動きが続く～

住宅投資は前期比▲0.2％と 2四半期連続の減
少を予測する。 
新設住宅着工戸数（季節調整済・年率換算値）
は、建築物省エネ法・建築基準法改正前の駆け
込み需要の反動で、2025年 1-3月期の 86.7万戸
から 4-6月期に 62.0万戸へ急減した後、7-9月
が 71.9万戸と持ち直し、その後は概ね 70万戸
台半ばで推移している。 
GDP統計の住宅投資は住宅価格上昇の影響も
あり弱めの動きとなっている。

・民間設備投資～高水準の企業収益を背景に増加～

民間設備投資は前期比 1.4％と 3四半期ぶりの増加を予測する。 
設備投資の一致指標である資本財総供給は 2026年 4-6月期の前期比 4.9％の後、7-9月期も同 4.9％
となった。また、機械投資の先行指標である機械受注（船舶・電力を除く民需）は 2026年 4-6月
期に前期比 0.2％と 3四半期連続で増加した後、7、8月の平均は 4-6月期を 0.4％上回っている。 
日銀短観 2026年 9月調査では、2026年度の設備投資計画（含むソフトウェア・研究開発投資額、
除く土地投資額）が 6月調査から 0.5％上方修正され、前年度比 9.3％（全規模・全産業）となった。 
設備投資は、高水準の企業収益を背景に、人手不足対応の省力化投資、デジタル化に向けた情報

2026年度

・公的固定資本形成～2四半期連続の減少～

公的固定資本形成は、前期比▲2.4％と予測する。 
公共工事の先行指標である公共工事請負金額
は 2026年 7-9月期に前年比▲2.6％と 6四半期ぶ
りに減少した。また、公共工事の進捗を反映する
公共工事出来高（建設総合統計）は、2026年 4-6
月期に前年比 5.2％と 9四半期連続で増加した後、
2026年 7、8月の平均は同 2.4％と伸びが鈍化し
ている。 
2025年度補正予算による押し上げ効果が弱ま
っていることに加え、2026 年度当初予算の公共
事業関係費の伸びが抑えられていることから、弱
めの動きとなっている。

・外需～4四半期ぶりのマイナス寄与～

外需寄与度は前期比▲0.4％（前期比年率▲1.8％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナスを予測する。財貨・
サービスの輸出が前期比 2.0％、財貨・サービスの輸入が同 4.8％となるだろう。代替調達の進展に
伴う原油輸入量の急増を主因として輸入が高い伸
びとなり、輸出の伸びを上回ったことから、外需は
成長率の押し下げ要因となった。 
2026年 7-9月期の地域別輸出数量指数を季節調整
値（当研究所による試算値）でみると、米国向けが
前期比 0.9％（4-6月期：同 2.8％）、EU向けが前期
比▲2.4％（4-6月期：同 0.3％）、アジア向けが前期
比▲0.3％（4-6 月期：同▲0.8％）、うち中国向けが

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
4Q26 iPhone builds unchanged: We maintain our 4Q26 iPhone build forecast at
72M units (+33% q/q, -5% y/y). While builds are expected to increase sequentially,
volumes remain below last year’s level, with softer legacy-model production,
including the iPhone 16/17e, weighing on the outlook. Our 2H26 build forecast for

the new iPhone 18 family remains unchanged at 71M units (-21% y/y), with the y/y
comparison also affected by one fewer SKU launched than last year. Within this, we
continue to expect 7M units of iPhone Duo builds. We will continue to monitor the
production ramp and sell-through data for potential upside or downside to our build
estimates. Our full-year forecast implies CY26 iPhone shipments of 234M units
(+3% y/y), with modest growth for the year despite the softer 4Q y/y outlook.

4Q26 iPad builds unchanged; year-end production outlook remains subdued: We
maintain our 4Q26 iPad build forecast at 10.3M units (-10% q/q, -26% y/y), implying
continued pressure on production volumes into year-end. Our full-year estimate
remains 46.8M units (-14% y/y), with the sharper y/y contraction in 4Q pointing to a
weaker finish to the year.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

公司簡介 
上品為從事氟素樹脂材料相關製品之生產
廠商，主要經營氟素樹脂材料（鐵氟龍）相
關製品，包括氟素原料加工之半成品及成品
生產及銷售，下游應用廣泛，是全球氟素樹
脂加工應用廠商中少數能同時滿足各階段
設備、製造、研發、創新、設計等完整服務
的企業。3Q26 產品營收比重，氟素內襯設
備營收占 69.7% ， 氟 素 內 襯 管 件 類 占
10.2%，氟素應用材料類占 16.5 %，氟素
工程收入占 3.7%。3Q26 公司營收按地區
別區分，台灣占 47.4%，中國占 48.2%，美
國占 0.5%，其他亞洲占 1.6%，歐洲占
2.2%。

主要客戶： 
主要競爭對手：

葉家宏 bill.yeh@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

上品 (4770 TT) 
在手訂單金額提升，持續擴充產能因應

永豐觀點 
上品在手訂單金額提升至 80 億元，隨著先前低毛利訂單陸續消化完畢，產品組合
預期改善，公司規劃於 2027 持續進行產能擴充，後市展望持續正向，研究部維持
正向看待上品獲利表現。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議維持買進，主要係因：上品在手訂單金額持續提升至 80 億元，反映台灣
及中國地區接單表現持續暢旺，隨著公司低毛利訂單出貨完畢，產品組合應逐步好
轉，且公司規劃於 2027 持續擴充產能，挹注未來營收動能，研究部預估公司
2026/2027 稅後 EPS 14.55/23.01 元，參考歷史 PER 區間約位於 10-20X，以
2027 年預估 EPS，給予 15X PER 評價，目標價 345 元，投資建議維持買進。

ESG 評析 
上品企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學)

專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。

### Page 2

2

上品為從事氟素樹脂材料相關製品之生產廠商：公司成立於 1981 年，主要經營氟
素樹脂材料（鐵氟龍）相關製品，包括氟素原料加工之半成品及成品生產及銷售，
下游應用廣泛，是全球氟素樹脂加工應用廠商中少數能同時滿足各階段設備、製
造、研發、創新、設計等完整服務的企業。3Q26 產品營收比重，氟素內襯設備營
收占 69.7%，氟素內襯管件類占 10.2%，氟素應用材料類占 16.5 %，氟素工程收
入占 3.7%；3Q26 公司產品依應用類別區分，半導體占營收 95.1%，化工應用占
比為 3.1%，面板及太陽能應用占 1.4%，其他應用占 0.4%，3Q26 公司營收按地
區別區分，台灣占 47.4%，中國占 48.2%，美國占 0.5%，其他亞洲占 1.6%，歐
洲占 2.2%。 
3Q26 EPS 4.27 元，優於預期：上品 3Q26 營收 16 億元（+22.5% QoQ，+14.4% 
YoY），優於預期，主因上品於 2Q26 擴產，並持續拉升產能利用率，推升營收表
現，毛利率 34.64%（-1.91pts. QoQ，+4.91pts. YoY），低於預期，主因上品出
貨較多低毛利訂單，影響產品組合所致，稅後淨利 34.1 億元（+37.2% QoQ，
+39.9% YoY），優於預期，其中，營業費用低於預期，主因認列預期信用損失金
額降低所致，3Q26 稅後 EPS 4.27 元，優於預期。

### Page 3

3

在手訂單金額持續提升：上品在手訂單金額達 80 億元，較前次發報告時 75 億元
持續提升，其中，約 75%為彰濱廠訂單，約 25%為嘉興廠訂單，預期到年底在手
訂單金額將持續再提升。2026 接單槽車數量達 1,100 台，反映台灣化學品廠因應
晶圓廠大客戶需求而擴充產能，對於上品內襯設備及內襯管件需求同步增溫，而中
國記憶體廠及化學品廠亦持續擴充，下單動能有所增溫。隨著市況於 2026 大幅改
善，彰濱廠及嘉興廠已可挑選毛利率較佳的訂單進行承接，產品組合預期將逐步好
轉，有利其毛利率逐步回升。 
2027 上品計畫持續擴充產能：因應客戶需求強勁，公司於 2Q26 進行擴產，並持
續招工補充人力，拉升產能利用率，目前彰濱廠及嘉興廠已達近滿載水準，公司年
初彰濱廠槽車約當產能約為月產能 80 台，近期已提升至月產能 130 台，預期年底
將達滿載規模月產能 140 台，公司規劃將於 6M27 再擴充至月產能 180 台，嘉興
廠亦規劃提升產能約 30%，以因應客戶強勁需求。而先前承接的低毛利訂單仍有
部分尚未出貨完畢，預期將於 1Q27 出貨完畢，因營收比重持續下降，預期產品組
合應持續改善，有利毛利率及獲利表現回升。

後市展望正向，投資建議維持買進：展望後市，台灣化學品廠及中國記憶體、化學
品廠擴建，推升對上品氟素樹脂產品需求，上品在手訂單金額持續提升，隨著上品

率較佳的訂單進行承接，後市獲利展望正向。研究部預估公司 2026 營收 56.9 億

投資建議。

주가 추이

3Q26 매출액과 영업이익은 비현금성 비용 항목 개선으로 시장 기대치
를 상회했으나, 차량 판매 감소와 인플레이션 등으로 연간 가이던스는 
하향되었습니다. 올해 생산 차질에 따라 이연된 신차 판매를 ’27년에 
확대하며 매출/이익 회복을 가시화하는 부분에 주목 필요합니다. 
매출 성장은 금융부문이, 영업이익은 자동차부문이 기여 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 45.1조원(-3.5% 이하 YoY), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%)을 기록하며 시장 기대치를 상회했다. 파업으로 인한 생산 차질
로 3Q 판매량 92.1만대(-11.3%)에 그치며 자동차부문 매출은 큰 폭으
로 감소(33조원, -10.0%)했으나, 금융부문 매출이 취급 자산 증가로 큰 
폭으로 성장(+27.8%)했다. 반면, 영업이익은 금리 상승에 따른 금융부문 
영업비용 확대 영향과 기타부문 실적 부진 발생과 달리 자동차부문은 전
년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소와 판매보증 충당부채 전입
액 감소 등 우호적 환율효과로 큰 폭의 증가세(+24.8%) 기록했다. 
’26년 가이던스 하향, 구조적 반등 요인은 아직 
3Q 실적 발표에서 주목할 부분은 가이던스 하향과 현금흐름이다. 연간 
판매목표를 연초 415.8만대에서 400만대로 하향했고 영업이익률 목표
는 6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 0.8%p 하향했으며, ’26년 투자계획은 
17.8조원에서 16조원으로 낮췄다. 특히, 투자계획 하향은 차량 판매 매
출 감소 대비 인센티브, 원자재 가격 상승 등으로 현금흐름이 악화된 
부분에 기인한 것으로 판단된다. 실적발표에서 전사적 원가 최적화 활
동 추진을 강조했으나, 이는 좀 더 장기적 측면에서 효과가 가시화될 
것으로 판단되며, 인플레이션 및 원화절상 상황 고려 시 구조적 반등은 
단기적으로 쉽지 않을 수 있다. 
절치부심의 2027년, 목표주가 600.000원 유지 
그러나 매크로 상황을 제외하면  ’26년 하반기 생산 차질에 따른 신차 
판매효과가 ’27년으로 이연된 것이라는 점에 주목 필요하다.   ‘북미 HEV, 유럽 
BEV’ 전략 하에서 엘란트라, 투싼 풀세인지, GV80, G80 등 고수익 HEV 차종 
중심 미국 수요 대응을 통해 Mix 개선하는 모습이 확인되고, 생산이 본격화된 
아이오닉3 유럽 판매 확대 시 중국 로컬업체들의 공세를 방어하는 모습을 
확인하는 것이 가능할 것으로 판단한다. 노후차종 중심 운영에 따른 판매 둔화 
이외에 아직 근원적 판매 경쟁력 저하로는 판단하지 않는다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10 월 30 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

## Document 247: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) ESS 수주 모멘텀 이어진다LG에너지솔루션(373220) ESS 수주 모멘텀 이어진다ESS 수주 모멘텀 이어진다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) ESS 수주 모멘텀 이어진다LG에너지솔루션(373220) ESS 수주 모멘텀 이어진다ESS 수주 모멘텀 이어진다 - 기업.pdf

2026.11.04   
LG에너지솔루션 
(373220)

ESS 수주 모멘텀 이어진다 Company Brief

3Q26 실적, 원-달러 환율 하락에도 시장 기대치 대폭 상회 
동사의 3Q26 실적은 매출 9.6조원(+59% YoY, +28% QoQ), 영업이익 7,560억원 
(+26% YoY, +568% QoQ)으로 원/달러 환율 하락에도 불구하고 시장기대치(매출 
8.8조원, 영업이익 3,217억원)를 크게 상회했다. 당사는 Stellantis와의 캐나다 
합작관계 종료에 따른 보상금 등을 포함해 약 4,250억원의 일회성 이익이 반영된 
것으로 추정한다. 이를 제외한 영업이익은 약 3,310억원으로, 시장기대치에 대체로 
부합한 것으로 판단된다. AMPC는 미국 ESS 생산량 증가와 GM 얼티엄셀즈 재가동에 
힘입어 전분기 대비 약 73% 증가한 4,169억원을 기록했다. 사업부문별로는 ESS의 
실적 개선이 두드러졌다. 상반기 배터리 패키징 병목으로 이연됐던 물량 출하가 
집중되면서 ESS 출하량은 전분기 대비 60% 가량 증가한 것으로 추정된다. 자동차 
전지도 유럽향 물량 회복과 GM 얼티엄셀즈 재가동으로 매출이 전분기 대비 약 
33%로 증가했으며, 고정비 부담 완화에 따라 적자폭도 뚜렷하게 축소된 것으로 
판단된다. 소형전지는 주요 고객사인 Tesla의 판매 호조에 따른 출하 증가로 매출 
회복과 수익성을 뒷받침한 것으로 보인다. 
4Q26 ESS 중심의 수익성 개선과 수주 모멘텀 지속될 전망 
미국 ESS 시장은 신재생 에너지 확대와 AI 데이터센터의 전력 수요 증가에 힘입어 
빠르게 성장하고 있다. 특히 정책 환경도 비PFE 공급망을 확보한 업체에 유리하게 
작용하고 있다. 동사는 3Q26 실적 컨퍼런스 콜에서 ESS 수주잔고가 약 
160GWh이며, 2028년까지 캐파의 대부분에 대응하는 물량을 확보했다고 밝혔다. 
또한 현재 다수의 고객사들과 2028년 이후 공급 물량을 협의하고 있어 연말까지 
추가적인 수주 모멘텀이 이어질 것으로 예상된다. 이를 바탕으로 4Q26에도 동사의 
본업 실적은 ESS 중심의 개선세를 이어갈 전망이다. 전분기에 반영된 대규모 일회성 
이익의 기저효과로 영업이익은 감소하겠지만, 이를 제외하면 출하량 증가와 가동률 
상승에 따른 점진적인 수익성 개선이 예상된다. 수익성 중심의 사업 효율화를 위해 
소형 IT용 파우치 사업을 축소하고 있다는 점도 중장기 손익 개선에 긍정적이다. 이를 
반영한 4Q26 매출과 영업이익은 각각 약 10.5조원(+63% YoY, +9% QoQ), 
4,150억원(흑자전환 YoY, -45% QoQ)으로 추정된다. 
매수 투자의견과 목표주가 유지 
동사에 대한 매수 투자의견과 목표주가를 유지한다. 북미 ESS 수요 증가와 공급망 
재편에 따른 신규 수주 확대가 중장기 실적 성장을 뒷받침할 전망이다. 특히 ESS용 
생산라인 전환과 가동률 상승이 본격화되면 매출 증가에 더해 고정비 부담 완화와 
AMPC 수혜 확대가 맞물리면서 수익성도 개선될 것으로 예상된다. ESS의 역할이 
신재생 에너지 연계용 저장장치를 넘어 AI 데이터센터의 안정적인 전력 공급을 
지원하는 핵심 인프라로 확대되고 있다는 점에도 주목할 필요가 있다. 이에 따라 
동사의 밸류에이션 역시 기존 EV 중심의 평가에서 벗어나 전력 인프라 산업의 
성장성을 반영하는 방향으로 재평가되어야 한다. 업종 내 실적 개선 가시성과 정책 
수혜가 뚜렷하고, 4Q26 신규 수주에 따른 주가 상승 가능성도 높다는 점에서 동사에 
대한 비중 확대 전략이 유효하다는 판단이다. 
Buy (Maintain)

당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상 
산업추천 투자등급

 Overweight(비중확대) 
 Neutral(중립) 
 Underweight(비중축소)

## Document 250: [402340] SK스퀘어(402340) SK하이닉스 최대주주SK스퀘어(402340) SK하이닉스 최대주주SK하이닉스 최대주주 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[402340] SK스퀘어(402340) SK하이닉스 최대주주SK스퀘어(402340) SK하이닉스 최대주주SK하이닉스 최대주주 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

SK 스퀘어 (402340/KS) 
SK 하이닉스 최대주주 
SK 하이닉스 4 분기도 배당 주당 20,000 원 이상  
SK 하이닉스는 전일 주당 20,000 원(실적반영: 10,000 원, 키옥시아 특별배당: 
10,000원)의 3분기 배당을 발표하였다. 키옥시아 특별배당은 3분기 배당과 26년 회
계연도 결산배당으로 나누어 지급예정이다. 이에 SK 스퀘어는 2조 9,220억원(1분기
와 2분기 배당수입은 각 548억원)의 배당수입을 수취하게 된다. SK하이닉스는 26년 
전체 현금 배당 규모는 30조원 이상을 발표했는데, 3분기까지 지급하는 배당금은 14.6
조원이기 때문에 4분기에도 최소한 주당 20.000원 이상의 배당이 예상된다. 이 경우 
SK 스퀘어의 26 년 배당수입은 6 조원에 달할 것으로 예상한다. SK 스퀘어는 ‘26 년
~’28년 경상배당수입의 30% 이상을 주주에게 환원하는 주주환원정책을 보유하고 있
어 SK스퀘어의 현 시가총액 대비 27년 주주환원율은 최소 1.5% 이상 될 전망이다.

SK 하이닉스 자사주 취득·소각으로 높아질 지배력 
SK하이닉스는 20조원의 자사주 매입을 발표하였다. 이 중 10조원은 임직원 보상목적
이며, 10조원은 주주환원 목적이다. 26년 총 60조원 이상의 자사주 취득·소각 예정(8
월 결의분 40 조원 포함)으로 임직원 보상 목적의 자사주 매입은 제외되기 때문에 26
년 실적 확정 후 추가적으로 10조원 이상의 자사주 취득이 예상된다. 기 취득한 자사주 
40조원(3.3%)는 11월 5일 소각 예정으로 소각 이후 SK스퀘어의 SK하이닉스 지분
율은 20.5%(2 분기말 기준)에서 21.2%로 상승하게 된다. 이번 발표한 10 조원
(1.0%) 자사주 소각시 SK 스퀘어의 지분율은 21.4%이 되며, 추가로 10 조원을 추가 
소각한다면 21.7%까지 지분율이 상승하게 된다.

SK 하이닉스 대비 높은 베타 
SK스퀘어에 대한 투자의견 매수를 유지한다. SK스퀘어 NAV의 98%가량을 차지하
는 SK하이닉스의 현금배당 증가로 SK스퀘어의 주주환원 재원이 확대되고, SK하이닉
스의 자사주 취득·소각으로 지배력이 강화되기 때문이다. SK하이닉스는 전일 컨퍼런스 
콜을 통해 29년까지 D램과 HBM 물량이 사실상 모두 솔드아웃된 상황이라 밝히며 주
가 반등 가능성이 높은데, SK 스퀘어 베타(1.8)는 SK 하이닉스(1.7) 대비 높기 때문에 
SK 하이닉스 주가 반등에 따른 SK 스퀘어의 동반 주가 상승을 기대할 수 있다. 현재 
SK스퀘어의 NAV 대비 할인율은 46.5%이며, SK하이닉스가 자사주 60조원 소각시 
할인율은 49.4%까지 상승하게 된다.

2026-10-29

지주회사/통신서비스/ESG. 최관순 / ks1.choi@sks.co.kr / 3773-8812

## Document 256: AI交易大跌后，汇丰：科技股已在定价“最坏情景”，2027年或迎“现金流翻身仗”

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI交易大跌后，汇丰：科技股已在定价“最坏情景”，2027年或迎“现金流翻身仗”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-26
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

汇丰认为，2026年全球股市尤其是AI板块的深度回调已将估值压⾄“最坏情景”。随着云⼚
商⾃由现⾦流将于2027年上半年⻅底回升，叠加AI变现担忧、美债收益率及能源价格“三⼤
⻅顶”，2027年股市⻛险回报⽐显著改善，标普500每股盈利预计增⻓17%。
摘要：
汇丰认为，全球股市尤其是AI板块在2026年的深度回撤，已经把估值压到“最坏情景”的⽔平。随
着超⼤规模云⼚商⾃由现⾦流在2027年⼀季度或⼆季度⻅底回升、债券收益率⻅顶以及美联储
紧缩⼒度不及预期，2027年股市的⻛险回报⽐已显著改善。
据追⻛交易台消息，这家投⾏的全球股票策略主管Alastair Pinder在11⽉24⽇发布的《2027年
全球股票展望》中表⽰，标普500指数每股盈利2027年预计增⻓17%，全球科技股当前估值所隐
含的中期EPS增速已接近零，⽽2025年中期这⼀数字还是25%。他判断，只要此前压制估值的
三⼤压⼒不再加剧，市场就有望迎来重估。
这⼀判断的核⼼催化剂是现⾦流拐点。汇丰预计，随着云收⼊加速和资本开⽀上调放缓，美国

超⼤规模云⼚商的⾃由现⾦流将在2027年上半年⻅底，这被视为打消“AI变现”疑虑的关键信
号。与此同时，市场已计⼊美联储到2027年中期再加息65个基点，汇丰认为进⼀步偏鹰的空间
有限。
在配置上，汇丰超配美国和新兴市场，中性看待欧洲，低配⽇本；⾏业层⾯偏好科技尤其是半
导体，同时看多⾦融、⼯业和材料等周期板块。该⾏强调，在盈利增速集中、AI⽣态循环性上升
的背景下，资产负债表质量⽐以往更重要。
估值已计⼊“最坏情景”
2026年全球股市的下跌主要集中在估值端。据汇丰统计，MSCI全球基准指数（FTSE All-
World）的12个⽉远期市盈率今年以来下降25%，标普500的远期市盈率较2025年末⾼点下滑
28%，近20%的成分股估值收缩超过30%，另有15%收缩20%⾄30%。
根据其折现盈利模型倒推，标普500当前估值所隐含的中期（FY2⾄FY5）EPS增速已从2025年
中期的16%复合增速降⾄7%，⼤致回到⼗年均值附近；全球科技股隐含的中期增速则从25%骤
降⾄2%左右。汇丰称，从估值⻆度看，市场已在为“最坏情景”定价。

回撤的⼴度同样显著。新兴市场个股平均较52周⾼点低35%，为2010年以来最深的回撤之⼀；
标普500个股平均下跌22%。美国10年期国债收益率升⾄约5%，为2007年以来最⾼。
汇丰称，估值每进⼀步上⾏50个基点，合理市盈率就会被压缩4.3%。不过，其固收团队认为利
率或已⻅顶，预计到2027年底美国10年期收益率为4.75%。
三⼤“⻅顶”构成重估催化剂
汇丰提出，2027年将出现三个“⻅顶”：AI变现担忧⻅顶、债券收益率⻅顶、能源价格⻅顶。这
三者同时转向，是推动估值重估的基础。
在AI⽅⾯，市场此前的担忧集中在基础设施供给增速快于可⽀撑的收⼊。据汇丰统计，市场对
2026年⾄2030年超⼤规模云⼚商资本开⽀的⼀致预期合计达5.5万亿美元，年均1.1万亿美元，
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

另有2.5万亿美元的未开始租赁和采购承诺。该⾏认为，2027年将证明AI需求之强以及这些资本
开⽀的变现能⼒。
其依据包括：前沿模型需求依然强劲，⽽企业AI⽀出仍处低位。据路透10⽉8⽇报道，Anthropic
最新IPO⽂件显⽰，其10⽉年化收⼊运⾏率达到810亿美元；OpenAI在9⽉达到500亿美元。
Ramp数据显⽰，企业AI⽀出中位数为每名员⼯每⽉16.3美元，较5⽉增⻓60%，⽽⽀出最⾼的
10%企业为800美元，最⾼的1%为1万美元。
汇丰测算，若标普500企业平均按每名员⼯每⽉800美元⽀出，年⽀出将达3050亿美元，相当于
标普500 EBITDA的6.2%、⼯资总额的10%、研发⽀出的33%。据Cledara数据，AI⽬前仅占软
件预算的25%，却获得新增IT及软件预算的50%。汇丰科技团队预计，到2030年AI⼤模型⽣态
年收⼊将达到约9200亿美元。
超⼤规模⼚商：现⾦流拐点是最重要催化剂
汇丰认为，美国超⼤规模云⼚商拥有该板块中最强的商业模式。它们既从算⼒供给中获取收

⼊，⼜从平台上的模型调⽤中抽取费⽤。Anthropic的S-1⽂件显⽰，其平台交易抽成约16%，且
不计算⼒成本。
算⼒定价显⽰需求仍超过供给。尽管产能快速扩张，英伟达B200和H100的租赁价格仍在上涨。
微软、亚⻢逊、Alphabet和甲⻣⽂的剩余履约义务（RPO）在2026年三季度达到2.5万亿美元，
⾼于2025年⼆季度的8150亿美元。市场预计，亚⻢逊、微软和Alphabet合计云收⼊将从2026年
⼆季度年化4000亿美元，增⾄2028年四季度近9250亿美元。
资本开⽀上调已开始放缓。最近⼀个财报季中，超⼤规模⼚商2027年资本开⽀⼀致预期上调550
亿美元，上⼀季度为1450亿美元。汇丰认为，上调幅度的峰值已经过去，这意味着⾃由现⾦流
预测⾯临的下⾏压⼒将减轻，市场⼀致预期已指向2027年⼀季度或⼆季度现⾦流拐点。
该⾏同时提⽰了数据中⼼的政治阻⼒。⺠主党在得州和俄亥俄州胜选后，汇丰估算，美国已规
划或在建的310吉⽡数据中⼼容量中，约80%位于⺠主党州⻓主政的州，中期选举前为35%。不

过，该⾏认为选举不太可能阻断建设，更可能的结果是审批时间拉⻓、并⽹成本更多由云⼚商
承担。得州当选州⻓Gina Hinojosa表⽰，其提出的暂停令仅适⽤于仍在等待州许可的项⽬。
Meta总裁在选举后不久称，已有⼀位即将上任的州⻓要求会⾯，讨论留住本州的数据中⼼投资
（据《华盛顿观察家报》11⽉5⽇报道）。

### Page 3

半导体：估值⼤幅重置，盈利可⻅性更好
在科技板块内部，汇丰继续偏好受益于资本开⽀周期的“卖铲⼈”。据彭博供应链数据，2027年超
⼤规模⼚商资本开⽀中近78%将流向半导体和AI服务器。经历近期抛售后，美国半导体股对应
12个⽉远期盈利仅9.8倍，⾃6⽉以来估值收缩60%。
汇丰认为，半导体企业直接受益于训练和推理量的增⻓，收⼊对单⼀模型或应⽤的最终成败依
赖较低，叠加资本开⽀计划仍处⾼位，其盈利可⻅性优于许多最终承担变现任务的公司。
存储⽅⾯，汇丰认为估值已在计⼊盈利快速周期性下滑。盈利动能虽仍为正但已开始放缓。但
该⾏认为短期威胁被⾼估：⻓期协议的普及提供了远⾼于以往周期的下⾏保护。
以美光为例，其已签署26份照付不议协议，覆盖到2030年预期收⼊的35%以上。其中四分之三
的收⼊已确定定价，主要通过价格上下限设定；16份协议中有14份对应约1000亿美元的最低价
格累计收⼊。管理层预计，在价格下限情形下⽑利率仍将超过以往周期峰值。汇丰称，DRAM价
格是近期最关键的指标，在存储股下跌期间始终坚挺，尚未出现估值所预期的价格疲软。

AI采⽤率提速，盈利上修已现
汇丰预计，随着更多企业在利润率与销售增⻓中体现⽣产⼒收益，AI采⽤将在2027年成为投资
主题。其基于财报电话会议样本的统计显⽰，美国约80%的企业已在讨论AI的实际⽤途，中国⼤
陆采⽤率也相对较⾼。
其数据科学团队⽤GPT-5对财报电话会议中的AI表述打分，1分仅停留在概念层⾯，5分需有可
量化的收益证据。美国企业中仅20%达到这⼀较⾼⻔槛，但⽐例在快速上升。
在盈利上修上已出现分化。汇丰将评分为4分或5分的企业归为“AI采⽤者”，这些企业2026年
EPS预测的上修幅度在美国、欧洲和⽇本分别⾼出其余样本2、1.5和1个百分点，主要来⾃利润
率预测上调。
该⾏列⽰的案例包括：美国西⽅⽯油称，对约9000⼝井的AI监测使年产量最⾼提升7%、故障率
下降22%；欧洲诺基亚称其AI⽹络⾃动化⼯具将⽹络中断时间削减96%；新兴市场中通快递
称，2026年上半年运输成本节约中约10%归功于AI；⽇本索尼称，过去五年AI⽤于PlayStation
⽀付路由带来超过7亿美元的增量收⼊。

盈利：2027年增⻓17%，但集中度是⻛险
汇丰预计2027年盈利仍将稳健。市场⼀致预期标普500 2027年EPS增⻓17%，低于2026年的
32%，但后者包含微软、Alphabet和亚⻢逊对Anthropic与OpenAI投资的⼀次性重估收益——

### Page 4

2026年上半年这三家公司其他净收⼊合计因此增加2000亿美元。剔除这⼀因素，2027年盈利增
速将接近25%。
增⻓⾼度集中。仅英伟达和美光两家就预计贡献标普500 EPS增⻓的约50%，汇丰直⾔这本⾝
就是⻛险。此外，对英伟达的独⽴调查和国会审查可能导致出⼝限制收紧。
除集中度外，其余市场增⻓依然稳健。标普500成分股EPS增速中位数预计约10%，与2026年⼤
致持平。⽇本2027年盈利预计增⻓17%，MSCI新兴市场指数（不含韩国）预计增⻓16%，欧洲
约为10%。
配置：超配美国与新兴市场，偏好周期与⾼质量
区域配置上，汇丰超配美国，理由是盈利领先；超配新兴市场，理由是外资流出创纪录后估值
吸引⼒上升；中性看待欧洲，其盈利仍有韧性但增⻓落后，且相对美国的估值折价已收窄，不
过该⾏看好欧洲的⾦融和⼯业等周期板块；低配⽇本，其盈利增⻓仍强但相对其他国际市场偏
贵，且⽇本央⾏若⽐预期更鸽派，可能令⽇元承压。
⾏业上，汇丰超配科技、⾦融、⼯业和材料，低配必需消费、电信和公⽤事业，对可选消费、
医疗、能源和房地产持中性。该⾏表⽰，增⻓仍具韧性，因此偏向周期股，并更看好⼯业与材
料⽽⾮消费板块；在2027年前期尤其偏好资产负债表强劲的公司。

### Page 6

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

## Document 297: Capital Market Outlook (2026-11-23)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Capital Market Outlook (2026-11-23).pdf
Report source: Merrill Lynch (CMO)
Date: 2026-11-23
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 November 23, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
AI Implies Dramatic Shift in the Structure of the U.S. Economy
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
On November 16, Federal Reserve Chairman Kevin Warsh moderated three conference
sessions at a symposium focused on how AI will ripple through the economy. The event
was the first public look at one of the five task forces Warsh created to review the
Federal Reserve (Fed) processes for doing its job. The task forces are meant to help

reform the Fed by building a consensus for overhaul to address shortcomings Mr. Warsh
has raised about the Fed's performance over the years. The outsized role of AI in the
evolution of the U.S. economy is reflected in the task force to consider AI's impact on
employment and productivity. This is, perhaps, the least controversial of the various
issues the task forces are charged with considering but nevertheless one of major
importance.
The main takeaway from the symposium was that various scenarios remain possible

with quite different implications for the future of U.S. growth, employment and interest
rates. The wide range of outcomes has contributed to the recent volatility in equity
markets, especially the parts of the market most closely associated with the buildout
of AI data centers. Estimating the payoff from the buildout naturally remains a key
focus for investors but one that is subject to uncertainty because of the rapid pace of
technological innovation and the political pressures that are arising from public concerns

over the changes AI is bringing.
Exhibit 1 helps shed light on the dramatic consequences AI is likely to create for the
structure of the U.S. economy and employment. The cost structure of corporate output
as of 2025 includes about half of each dollar for labor. An estimate for the cost structure
of a dollar spent on inferential AI after the data center buildout reaches a net stock
centered around $5 trillion in 2030 requires only a fourth as much labor per unit of

revenue or output. The bigger the share of overall value inferential AI becomes in gross
domestic product (GDP) the lower the labor share of income will become. This is not a
new trend. The labor share of income has been gradually declining ever since the digital
economy began to develop over a half century ago. What is new is the speed of the
transformation. Self-generative AI is representative of the singularity, the point when the
spread of this digital transformation becomes exponential.

The drastically lower labor share of value implied by AI output implies that other costs
comprise bigger shares. For example, the exhibit shows the profit share of revenue is
much higher (28% vs.19%) for AI-generated output. This is already evident in the profit
profile of the S&P 500 where the margins and profit growth of the AI-related companies
are orders of magnitude greater than the profit profiles of non-AI-related businesses.
Presumably, as the old economy increasingly transforms to this new economy cost

structure, the productivity benefits of AI will increasingly transfer over to traditional
businesses and transform their cost structures in the direction of those of inferential AI
builders. This analysis suggests profit growth is moving toward a bigger share of output
over the next few years.
Competition naturally eats away at "excess" profits, those not justified by costs. Exhibit 1
partly illustrates the justification for higher AI profits. AI is capital-intensive compared

to the non-AI economy. This can be seen in the much higher depreciation-cost share
in the AI economy (28% vs.15%). AI capital is more short-lived than average, reflecting
the quicker obsolescence that naturally results from accelerating technological change
and less durable infrastructure (short-lived chips). This issue of rapid depreciation has
surfaced in the debate over potential AI profitability, with analysts trying to figure the
right life span of data centers to model their profit outlook.

Investment Implications
AI's creative destruction is likely to
leave a lot of laggards in its wake while
creating a stronger, more profitable
economy for investors. As a result,
we believe a diversified investment
approach favoring high-quality balance
sheets and reliable free cash flows is
warranted.

### Page 3

3 of 8 November 23, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Finally, "other costs" make up about a third of AI output cost compared to just 12% for
non-AI output. Among these are the increased power costs associated with intensive
compute processing. Electricity costs are going to replace a lot of labor costs. As the
midterms made clear, rules and regulation for data centers, including power and water
issues, are likely to come under much greater scrutiny as well as other environmental and

"quality-of-life" issues.
Ultimately, this new cost structure for the AI economy will be the focus of innovation
to bring down costs and remove bottlenecks. Technology is ultimately deflationary.
Monetary policy is the cause of inflation. This "deflationary" or declining cost aspect
of technological progress is a key factor behind the notion that the promise of the
singularity is a world of superabundance. Intelligence, power and compute costs are likely

to become cheaper and cheaper as innovators work to create this superabundance.
Exhibit 1: Comparison of Corporate Cost Structure to Produce 2025 Output to
Corporate Cost Structure to Produce Inference Compute Revenue from AI Data

Employment and AI. The dramatic reduction in labor needed to run an AI cost structure
promises a much higher level of productivity and therefore higher levels of wealth and
income. As the cost structure in Exhibit 1 illustrates, however, a much bigger share
of output goes to capital and therefore shareholders in an AI-dominated economic
structure. There are various ways this change could sort itself out. In a society with an
increasingly older population, more capital income solves the problem of more retirees

needing to live on investment income. AI also promises to increase longevity. Therefore,
the future is likely to include more elderly people living on capital income as well as
more elderly people working. In addition, the bounty from higher productivity is likely
to support demand for new goods and services as past technology breakthroughs have
shown. People will be required to produce those new goods and services.
Nevertheless, it's clear from the distribution of capital income that's becoming more, not

less, concentrated, that policies that spread wealth ownership across a broader swath
of the population could potentially help ease the social tensions plaguing the population
under the guise of "affordability." The proliferation of gifts from the wealthy to Trump
Accounts is a reflection of the recognition that this issue needs to be addressed. The
U.S. Treasury rolled out temporary regulations at the end of September that allow Trump
Accounts to hold donated individual stocks instead of just diversified index funds. There

is likely to be a push to expand the growth of large philanthropic donations from the
private sector to fund retirement-type accounts for the broader population. This is an
alternative to higher tax rates that fund government spending rather than long-term
saving. A society that shares more broadly in the fruits of AI is more likely to tolerate AI's
profound changes.

### Page 4

4 of 8 November 23, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Too Connected to Fail: When the Jetsons Meet the Flintstones
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
This month, when a leading U.S. technology company went dark, planes across America
were grounded, and major banks went offline, investors learned an unsettling lesson on
the fragility of America’s digital economy.  We learned that interconnectivity is both a

strength and a weakness—and that the digital economy of the future rests upon the
shaky infrastructure of the past.
We live in a world where AI is advancing at warp speed. Computing power is expanding
exponentially. Financial transactions move around the globe in milliseconds. And cloud
computing keeps billions of people and businesses connected every day. It is a world
where everything is faster, digital and connected.
The U.S. digital economy now accounts for roughly 12% of GDP, expanding about three

times faster than the economy overall this century. Digital gross output totaled $3.7
trillion in 2021, the last year of available data from the Commerce Department; that said,
the figure almost certainly understates the current size of the U.S. digital economy. Digital
commerce now accounts for nearly 18% of all retail sales ($350 billion), although again,
this number is understated. Add in business-to-business digital transactions and digitally

delivered professional services, and the amount of daily digital commerce in the U.S. is
massive and growing robustly. Each one of these transactions ultimately depends on
communications services, electricity, computing facilities, and digital payments networks.
Yet here’s the rub: our digital world is juxtaposed against a physical infrastructure
of aging radar systems, antiquated telecommunications equipment, half-century-old
transmission lines, vulnerable underwater subsea cables and decades-old software.

Here we are, well into the 21st century, and a handful of Federal Aviation Administration
facilities are still using floppy disks to support aviation travel. Our world is a world where
the space-age Jetsons have collided with the Stone Age Flintstones.
Something had to give, and it did this November: business communications were
disrupted, planes were grounded, ATMs were frozen and investors were left wondering
“what’s next”?
In our opinion, “what’s next” entails a renewed focus and commitment on the part of the

U.S. public and private sectors to build America’s physical infrastructure back better and
faster. The recent disruptions, we believe, are a reminder that a nation’s infrastructure is
essentially its backbone, and every nation, like every human being, is only as strong as its
skeletal framework.
A healthy backbone allows an economy to move, pivot, grow, innovate and compete. An
impaired spine, in contrast, constrains mobility, undermines nimbleness and flexibility and

is prone to setbacks.
So, think of roads, bridges, ports, airports, electrical grids, communication networks and,
increasingly, digital networks not merely as conveniences of modern life. Rather, think
of them as foundational components of economic activity, national security and global
competitiveness. Without them, even the world’s most sophisticated economy can be
brought to a standstill.
The great irony—and potential opportunity—of the “too connected to fail” is that while the

U.S. leads the world in AI, cloud computing, and other advanced technological capabilities,
the physical infrastructure of the nation continues to show its age. Electrical grids are
struggling to accommodate increasing demand. Transmission networks are plagued with
bottlenecks. Aviation communications systems built in the 1970s are overwhelmed.
None of the above is new—America’s infrastructure deficiencies have been widely
acknowledged but frequently tolerated for decades. The grid held up most of the time,

while airline safety remained relatively stellar. Power outages during storms and heat
waves were expected and tolerated—no matter the misery created. All good.
Investment Implications
The future is digital, which
requires more capital investment
on America’s aging physical
infrastructure. We continue to favor
Industrials, infrastructure-related
firms, and commodities for the long-
run.

### Page 5

5 of 8 November 23, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Today, however, it’s different. Because there is so much riding on our digital
connectivity—think trillions of dollars of daily commerce—the cost of failure or
disruption continues to soar. Case in point: America’s recent aviation meltdown. The
communications malfunction triggered a national four-hour ground stop, resulting in
nearly 20,000 flight cancellations over four days, at a potential cost to the airlines

of more than $1 billion in profits. Adding more salt to the wound, fried and frizzled
passengers absorbed an estimated $1.9 billion in additional economic costs. All because
the communications infrastructure supporting the world’s most sophisticated aviation
system failed to handle a technical upgrade.
Where are the Jetsons when you need them? We believe the recent technological failures
will accelerate infrastructure spending for a number of reasons. One, as shown above,

the economic costs of digital failures are rising. Two, America’s digital economy is now
too big—and too connected—to fail. Three, the extraordinary power needed to run
the AI revolution makes it absolutely essential that the U.S. stop talking about fixing
its aging infrastructure and start doing it. Four, with the stakes so large for America’s
largest technology companies, with enormous incentives of their own, these firms are
contributing their private capital of their own to build back America’s infrastructure

faster and better. And fifth, an aging air traffic control system, an unreliable national
grid, and weathered transmission lines―these are more than just economic and financial
challenges. They are challenges to America’s national security—notably to the great
power rivalry between the U.S. and China. This rivalry, in our opinion, will help create
more bipartisan support for increased infrastructure spending and greater public-private
sector cooperation.

Investment summary. It’s taken a series of technological snafus, but here we are—
America’s infrastructure is gaining the attention—and capital—it needs. It’s also
garnered the attention of Congress. Just last week, the Bipartisan American Affordability
Act, which reduces permitting burdens on a number of projects, passed a key procedural
vote in the Senate. Whether the House votes in favor of the bill remains to be seen but
in general, the U.S.’s cumbersome permitting and regulatory systems are coming under

more scrutiny.
To this point, the bipartisan National Governors Association recently outlined
recommendations designed to cut through the permitting and regulatory red tape. The
Department of Energy has developed a coordinated federal transmission permitting
framework designed to clear and approve projects at a faster pace. Yes, it will take
some time to cut through the labyrinth of federal, state and local approvals, permits, and
regulations. But the need for reforms has become quite evident and urgent, with political

inaction less acceptable to an electorate increasingly worried and wary of the next
technology glitch.
So, who potentially benefits in our theme of “too connected to fail”?  In our opinion,
the winners will include electrical equipment manufacturers, engineering firms, utilities,
telecommunications infrastructure providers, industrial automation companies, and
specialized construction firms. Specialists in maritime surveillance, underwater sonar
detection, and fiber-optics sensing capabilities are well positioned in this new world.

In terms of asset allocation, this means considering more exposure to infrastructure-
related industrial companies and leaders in renewables (solar, wind, electric vehicles,
biomass) and the required infrastructure behind each renewable energy source. Leaders
in electricity distribution, charging stations, batteries, as well as low-carbon hydrogen,
biomethane, and advanced biofuels should also be considered in portfolios. Ditto for
leaders in low-carbon technologies (LED lighting, smart energy meters, and storage),

and leaders in transmission technologies like high-voltage direct current (HVDC), which
transfers wind and solar power from where it is created to where it is needed.  We also
still favor commodities over the long term, as well as cybersecurity leaders.

### Page 6

6 of 8 November 23, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
In our view, what does the market want and need to see to establish a firmer base?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Officer
IInvestors are looking to reprice uncertainty for 2027. A lot has been digested by this
market since semiconductor stocks peaked in June. Inflation, Fed policy, geopolitics,
the funding of the AI buildout, CEO changes and future power adjustments out of the

midterm elections all in a matter of months.
Most importantly, investors kept clinging to the base—earnings and profitability. As long
as the base remained strong and growing, investors were willing to take the risk of a
market led by technology and the AI buildout more specifically.
Recently, investors have shifted to wanting more certainty: certainty around higher-
quality, transparency in FCF, the ability to secure financing if needed, reasonable

valuations and strong moats that last beyond peak profitability. This is why we have
experienced a shift from the more highly cyclical semiconductors to the hyperscalers in
terms of positioning.
The market wants to see that AI spending remains robust but with more reasonable
expectations for 2027 and perhaps 2028. A reset of expectations by the leaders of
the buildout can go a long way to finalizing this “flush” stage, in our view. We remain
diversified Equity and are proud Bulls overall.

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

S&P 500/Market-cap Weighted Index is a market-capitalization-weighted index that is widely regarded as the best single gauge of large-cap U.S. equities. The index includes 500 leading
companies and covers approximately 80% of available market capitalization
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index is the equal-weight version of the widely-used S&P 500, in which each constituent company is given the same weight (approximately 0.2% each), rather than
weighting by market capitalization.

from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.

First Impressions: NZ retail trade, September quarter 2026 - New
Zealanders fill up the car and head back to the mall
By Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
09:19 November 23 2026 Share
The September quarter saw a larger than expected rise in spending, including firmness in
core categories.
September quarter retail sales

### Page 2

Westpac f/c: 1.4%, Market: +0.5% 
Core retail sales (volume of goods sold): 1.4% (Prev: 0.8%)

Nominal retail sales: 2.4% (Prev: 1.0%)

Year to September
Volume of goods sold: 3.5%
Nominal sales: 7.0%

New Zealanders fill up the car and head back to the mall.
The September quarter retail sales report showed a sharp recovery in spending. Nominal spending
was up 2.4% over the quarter, and the amount of goods that New Zealanders are actually taking home
was up a solid 2.2%.
Today’s result was above our top of market forecast for a 1.4% quarterly rise, and well above the
median market forecast for +0.5%.
While the strong September quarter result is welcome news, we are taking it with a grain of salt. Much
of the rise in spending was related to fuel spending, which was boosted by the fall in fuel prices over
the September quarter, and fuel prices have step higher since then.
However, even excluding fuel, we have seen firmness in spending appetites. Core spending (excl. fuel
and motor vehicles) was up 1.4% over the September quarter and is 4.7% higher than this time last
year. 
Looking at the breakdown of spending, we did see a rise in grocery purchases. Meanwhile, spending in
discretionary categories was mixed – there has been lower spending on items like furnishings and
hardware, while spending on hospitality, electronics and recreational items is up. Overall, were left with
a picture of gradually improving spending appetites.

Outlook
Today’s report reinforces our expectations that the economy is moving into stronger phase. We expect
that spending levels will continue to firm through the holiday season and 2027. However, that recovery
is still likely to be gradual due to the combination of ongoing high inflation, a soft labour market and
rising interest rates. We also expect the recovery will be uneven, with those regions with strong links to
the agricultural and tourism sectors outperforming metro regions, like Auckland and Wellington.

Implications for GDP growth
We’re forecasting GDP growth of 0.6% in the September quarter. Today’s result was above
expectations. We’ll take a closer look at how our forecast for GDP growth is shaping up over the next
couple of weeks as additional data on September quarter activity is released.

Satish Ranchhod, Senior Economist

► 投資人持續擔憂AI科技發展，儘管輝達財報
優於預期，然代理執行長強調資本配置紀律
後，近期市場焦點圍繞在未來數年AI資本支
出下修與獲利成長放緩風險，疊加Anthropic
上市募集鉅資後，股價亦牽動AI估值變化。
市場恐慌情緒持續擴大，加重科技相關類股
與美國費城半導體指數持續修正，跌勢亦擴

散至非科技類股，僅部分具防禦性質如必需
消費與公用事業類股跌幅較小。
► 川普與會APEC亞太經合會，並與中國國家
主席習近平及俄羅斯總統普丁會面，中國宣
布將出資2,000萬美元予APEC並強調亞太共
同體，然早前美國商務部工業暨安全局公告
先進半導體遠端存取安全立法，管制中國企
業透過中國以外第三地海外雲端服務取得先
進運算資源，美中兩大國對AI科技競爭態勢
白熱化，先進晶片出口管制趨嚴亦加重科技
潛在擾動，疊加聯準會緊縮貨幣政策路徑與
地緣衝突存不確定性，市場波動恐延續。

► 聯準會公布會議記錄內容仍顯鷹派，部分官
員認為若高通膨持續且出現升溫跡象，不排
除12月升息2碼可能性，然國際油價走跌支
持通膨預期下行，財政部進行20年期公債標
售後結果持穩，各天期美國公債殖利率多走
降，然信用利差仍受市場波動影響而擴大，
僅美國投資級債利差上行幅度低於公債殖利
率下行幅度，近期表現相對非投資級穩健。
► 聯準會會議記錄凸顯官員鷹派論調，強化明
年聯準會升息路徑，疊加AI投資與地緣局勢
存在不確定性，皆形成短期美元指數下方支
撐力道，同時加重非美主要貨幣上方壓力。
► 中東地緣衝突再起，伊朗透過無人機對美軍
雷達系統發動攻擊，另荷姆茲海峽發生船隻
遇襲事件，然美國能源資訊管理局(EIA)公布
上週美國商業原油庫存大增340萬桶並高於
市場預估，原油供過於求擔憂與風險偏好降
溫造成油價走弱，另聯準會升息預期與美元
走升，亦對金價帶來一定程度壓力。

輝達
股價(左軸) 預估本益比(右軸)
美元 倍數
資料來源：Bloomberg、凱基證券整理
► 輝達2027財年第三季財報(營收、EPS、數據
中心)超預期，營收較去年同期翻倍以上、季
增20%。資料中心營收單季季增21%至1,076
億美元，大規模雲端服務商貢獻600億。
► 董事會表示黃仁勳已辭職不會復任，交接存
在不確定性，對公司潛在影響：1)輝達動用
自身強勁獲利能力，支持AI業界，市場質疑
輝達收入持續性，接替者出現前，融資策略
可能偏保守；2) 事 件 引 發 加 強 出 口 管 制 憂
慮，新管理層或缺乏在政府遊說的能力，收
緊出口可能衝擊向亞洲出貨，拖累獲利。
► 公司EPS年增翻倍，毛利率維持75%，但預
估本益比從2024年40倍持續回落至現時不足
11倍，輝達若長期高速增長，市場應給予更
高估值。管理層變動令評價更低，但事件曝
光前，預估本益比已跌至約15倍。輝達AI訓

練與推理有領導地位，產能鎖定具優勢，但
市占率正流失予客製化晶片，疊加民主黨偏
向放慢AI發展，可能令未來晶片需求放慢。
輝達財報具韌性，交接存不確定性，市場早已不願提高評價

### Page 6

6
► Claude AI模型開發商Anthropic上市，IPO認
股價達83美元高於先前目標區間，加上私募
260億美元，總募資資金逾1,000億美元超過
SpaceX上市紀錄，凱基投顧預估Anthropic
成長動能來自：(1)訂閱與用量制帶動營收至
2030年複合成長近4成；(2)儘管市場擔憂降
價問題，然算力需求仍緊且Anthropic第一層
級付費Token市佔明顯高於其他AI模型開發
商，(3)算力成本認列早於營收，預估2027年
首季為獲利相對低點，2026年經濟FCF就轉
正且後續將逐年增長。
► Anthropic企業用戶淨收入留存率(NRR)高達
9倍，展現對企業客戶強大吸引力，未來最大
營收來源為企業將更多工作交付AI執行，而
非更多人訂閱AI，然仍須留意近期科技擾動
形塑AI短期逆風，後續風險觀察企業AI預算
是 否 下 滑( 市場終端需求) 、 第一層級付費
Token市佔比重是否快速下滑(AI模型市場地
位鬆動)及實收均價年減幅是否連2年高於推
論成本(降價開始由利潤吸收)等負面因素。

### Page 7

7
投資焦點
► 聯準會會議記錄顯示所有官員認為景氣若按
預期態勢發展，後續仍有升息必要，部分官
員擔憂通膨較先前更加頑固，應採取較原先
更緊縮的升息步伐，有兩位官員亦表示若通
膨朝不利方向發展，可能需升息2碼，利率市
場持續定價反映更鷹派貨幣政策路徑。
► 財政赤字方面，民主黨掌握國會兩院將進入
分立政府，可能阻止共和黨推出更多支出法
案，強制性財政支出佔總支出6成且佔比將續
升，而強制性支出僅能以立法縮減，民主黨
國會席次未達三分之二，任何立法將受總統
否決權鉗制，美國財政紀律難見明顯好轉。
► 考量聯準會升息預期持續、美國政府債務累
積和利息支出成為財政最大隱憂，以及明年
新債務上限協商將形塑美國公債市場焦點，
另AI發債潮和川普政策不確定性持續，研判
上述美國公債多項結構利空短期難消散，待
聯準會最快明年上半年停止升息且利率見高
後，再對美國公債進行配置。
多數聯準會官員言論維持鷹派論調市場對聯準會明年升息3碼預期仍在
聯準會認證景氣穩，高利率環境恐持續至明年上半年

提出AI有望提升美國長期成長潛力，但可能
伴隨職位替代、投資報酬落空與股票評價下
跌三種情境與風險
Bowman
(監管副主席&理事) 永久票委
目前不覺得有必要進一步行動，聯準會仍有
時間觀察金融情勢與經濟發展，再決定是否
需採取行動
Cook
(理事) 永久票委 供應衝擊影響超預期持久，過去幾年裡所見 到供應衝擊也越來越多
Jefferson
(副主席&理事) 永久票委

其認為任何政策調整應仔細研究數據趨勢、
聯準會可能需要更多時間判斷不斷變化的前
景和風險平衡
Powell
(理事) 永久票委
Williams
(紐約Fed) 永久票委
經濟強勁且通膨仍高，其認為緊縮貨幣政策
不用過於激進，但承認通膨正朝著錯誤方向
發展，聯準會貨幣政策仍有許多工作要做
Paulson
(費城Fed) 地區票委 為使通膨降溫，其贊成逐步上調利率水準
Barr
(理事) 永久票委 Fed可能需進一步調整貨幣政策，來及時應 對通膨風險
Kashkari
(明尼阿波利斯Fed) 地區票委 Fed將採取一切必要措施，使通膨率回落至 政策目標
Hammack
(克里夫蘭Fed) 地區票委 傾向於緩步穩定升息，但倘若通膨壓力惡化， 聯準會可能需要考慮加快升息腳步
Waller
(理事) 永久票委 通膨壓力由能源業擴散至服務業，若核心 CPI仍月增0.3%以上，主張12月應升息2碼
Logan
(達拉斯Fed) 地區票委 冬季升息步伐可能比秋季快，呼籲至少需再 升息2次

美國投資級新發債量，10億美元
投資焦點投資級債
► 聯準會確認景氣態勢仍強，近月美國科技大
廠蘋果與微軟聯袂發債融資，受大額債券供
給與市場風險偏好降溫影響，推升美國投資
級債殖利率持續揚升，預期明年AI資本支出
持續，整體美國投資級企業發債量將維持歷
史相對高位，債市投資仍以中短天期投資級
債為布局首選，待明年上半年利率升至高位
後，再逐步增加中長天期債部位配置。
► 美國大型企業獲利體現高成長動能，今年來
投資級 債企 業財 務 槓桿持 穩且信 用利 差微
升，體現在每單位槓桿利差僅略為上行，考

量債信基本面仍佳有望限制利差擴大幅度；
建議佈局A級至BBB級公司債、並聚焦風險
調整後正利差者如銀行、非必需消費、公用
事業和通訊產業鎖住好息，亦可留意能源、
必需消費、公用事業和資本設備與科技債相
關性較低產業分散風險，科技投資級債雖債
信體質穩健且違約風險有限，然逢大額發債
潮不建議交易，僅以持有至到期為主。
財務槓桿持穩而利差微升，反映在債信指標上AI企業持續融資加劇信用市場擾動
企業發債將持續，留意利率上行時鎖定好債
投資焦點

### Page 9

9
股市
 黃仁勳相關事件恐逐步強化美國對AI晶片出口管制、AI資料中心建設進程或將受到州政府緊縮政策
而造成評價面壓力並增添股價波動。短期可留意防禦股以增強抗震力以抵禦市場震盪。長期而言，
AI從全面看好轉向選股，且大優於小，市場波瀾過後產業長期正向趨勢未變。當前則可布局財務健
全度較高的高品質價值股，以獲取穩定報酬並降低市場波動影響。
 歐、日股可分散配置，利率走升有利銀行股。此外，隨財政政策支持，歐洲國防軍工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，AI從
全面看好轉向選股、且大優於小，市場波
瀾後長期正向趨勢未變，當前可布局財務
健全度較高的高品質價值股
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲國防
債市
 美伊談判走向反覆未明，油價維持高檔震盪。CPI物價高於預期，強化市場對聯準會升息步伐預估，
聯準會官員立場維持較高利率抑制通膨發展。建議以中短天期債券投資為主，可逢利率走升時布局
進行鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引力者，
包含金融、非必需消費、通訊及公用事業等產業。

債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、非必需消費、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 CPI通膨支撐升息預期，美元走勢偏強。
 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期偏鷹，日圓弱勢盤整。
美元高檔整理
商品
 美伊談判走向反覆，油價一度回落但再度反彈，短線仍偏震盪。
 去美元化支撐全球央行買盤，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
低接買盤流入，黃金中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書(或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用(境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金(不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施)；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

## Document 427: US Economics Analyst_ 2027 US Outlook_ The End of the Inflation Shocks

Source PDF: all report pdf/US Economics Analyst_ 2027 US Outlook_ The End of the Inflation Shocks.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 20
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The main economic surprise of 2026, the energy shock from the Iran war, has not n
prevented the US economy from growing at a healthy pace but has reversed 
progress on inflation.  The key drivers of growth and inflation in 2026—the war, 
tax cuts, tariffs, and AI price pressures—should all have less impact next year.  
We expect this to leave 2027 GDP growth at an above-consensus 2.4% and the 
labor market stable, while allowing core PCE inflation to fall substantially to 2.4%. 
Growth should follow the same pattern in 2027 as in 2026: solid consumption n
growth and strong business investment growth offset by drags from housing and 
trade.  Consumption should grow a bit over 2%, driven mainly by firmer and more 
evenly balanced real income growth as inflation moderates and the boosts from 
last year’s tax cuts and wealth effects diminish.  AI drives half of our 8% forecast 
for business investment growth but contributes only modestly to measured GDP 
growth because much of the equipment is imported or undercounted in the GDP 
statistics.  Housing investment will likely contract again as poor affordability 
continues to depress demand. 
Near-potential growth should keep the labor market stable in 2027.  This year n
began with fears of a looming AI-driven layoff wave, but the layoff rate instead 
remained historically low, and the labor market settled into a slightly softer 
low-hire, low-fire form of full employment where wage growth has stabilized at a 
subdued pace of about 3.5%.  We expect this equilibrium to last for another year 
before AI displacement begins to have a larger impact in subsequent years. 
While the near-term inflation outlook remains uncertain, we are more confident n
that inflation will fall substantially by late 2027 as the effects of tariffs, the Iran 
war energy shock, and AI price pressures diminish.  We see these three forces as 
the key to the outlook because the economy is not overheated at a 
macroeconomic level or even at an industry level outside of pockets tied to the 
AI boom, and high inflation does not appear to have become entrenched in 
expectations.  As a result, inflation should moderate as long as these forces 
subside and no new shocks emerge. 
The Fed is likely to remain firmly focused on inflation in 2027.  We forecast two n
more 25bp rate hikes in December 2026 and March 2027 to a peak of 
4.25-4.5%, followed by gradual cuts back to 3.25-3.5% starting in November, the 
final meeting of 2027, once inflation has declined meaningfully.  The FOMC will 
also discuss the conclusions of the task forces next year, but we expect any

### Page 2

institutional changes to be limited.  In particular, a large reduction in the size of the
Fed’s balance sheet is unlikely, though a shift in the composition of its assets is
possible.
The most obvious risk to our forecast of sturdy growth and a long-awaited decline in n
inflation is that the inflation shocks of the last two years, which we and most
forecasters did not anticipate, could last longer, or new shocks could arise.  We also
see downside risks to growth from the war, a longer period of higher interest rates,

or earlier and more rapid job displacement from AI.  But our best guess is that there
will be fewer big policy-driven macroeconomic shocks in 2027.

### Page 3

2027 US Outlook: The End of the Inflation Shocks

The main economic surprise of 2026, the energy shock from the Iran war, has not 
prevented the US economy from growing at a healthy pace.  We expect GDP growth of 
2.5% in 2026 Q4/Q4, in line with our forecast from our 2026 Outlook but above both 
consensus expectations at the time and our forecast after the war began.  In contrast, 
the combination of the energy shock and price pressures sparked by the AI investment 
boom are likely to leave core PCE inflation at 3.2% on a Q4/Q4 basis, a disappointment 
to the more optimistic expectation for a large decline that we and consensus held at the 
start of the year.

The key drivers of growth and inflation in 2026—the war, tax cuts, tariffs, and AI price 
pressures—should all have less incremental impact in 2027.  We expect this to leave 
2027 Q4/Q4 GDP growth at 2.4% (above consensus at 2.2%) and the labor market 
stable, while allowing core PCE inflation to fall to 2.4% (in line with consensus).

### Page 4

The Growth Outlook: Solid Consumption, Strong Business Investment, 
and Weak Housing Balance Out to Near-Potential GDP Growth 
Growth should follow the same pattern in 2027 as in 2026: solid consumption growth 
and strong business investment growth offset by drags from housing and trade.  We 
expect this to balance out to overall GDP growth that is a hair above our 2.3% estimate 
of potential growth.  Relative to consensus, our growth forecast is a touch higher on 
consumer spending, much higher on business investment, and notably weaker on 
housing and net exports.

### Page 5

The resilience of the US economy this year in the face of the energy shock has been 
particularly impressive in the case of consumer spending.  Despite a boost to headline 
inflation that has left real wage growth close to zero this year, consumer spending has 
grown at a strong pace, supported by tax refunds, a positive wealth effect from the 
stock market, and a modest decline in the saving rate. 
Our consumer outlook for 2027 is less complicated: we expect 2.2% growth in consumer 
spending driven mainly by stronger real labor income growth as inflation moderates, 
with special factors playing a more limited role.  We do not expect a meaningful change 
in the saving rate, which is likely close to 5% after accounting for revised spending and 
income data that are incorporated with a lag, putting it roughly in line with what one 
would expect at a time when household wealth is high and layoff risk is low. 
Both real income and consumer spending should grow more evenly across the income 
distribution in 2027.  While we noted last year that real income was actually more in line 
with pre-pandemic trends across income quintiles than the K-shaped narrative 
suggested, in 2026 income growth has been weaker for low-income families, due in part 
to fiscal policy changes and the unequal burden of the energy shock.  Income growth 
should even out in 2027, with a boost at the bottom from lower inflation, cost-of-living 
adjustments to government transfer payments, and the likely reversal of recent cuts to 
social spending.

AI will remain the dominant driver of business investment growth in 2027.  We estimate 
that spending on AI-related hardware and intellectual property will exceed $900bn 
annualized by end-2026 (a $550bn increase from 2025) and $1.4tn by end-2027 (a 
further $500bn increase), roughly 4% of GDP. 
The midterm election results should pose only modest risks to AI investment in 2027.  
We expect newly elected Democratic governors in states that account for over a third of 
planned US data center capacity to impose moratoria on new data centers.  But these 
are unlikely to be long-lived and should only affect projects not yet permitted, leaving 
the vast majority of planned 2027 construction unaffected.

Moderates and the Impact of This Year’s Special Factors Diminishes

### Page 6

The impact of AI on the official economic statistics will be smaller than company 
spending data suggest, for a few reasons.  First, spending will rise less in real than in 
nominal terms because technology goods prices are likely to rise further.  Second, 
semiconductor investment is undercounted in the business investment statistics.  Third, 
most of the technology goods used for AI are imported and are therefore netted out in 
the GDP calculation. 
Despite the undercounting, AI drives half of our 8% forecast for business investment 
growth in 2027.  Its impact on GDP growth is more nuanced: we estimate it will 
contribute 0.2pp to officially measured GDP growth; 0.7pp to true GDP growth, adding 
back undercounted semiconductor investment and exports of chip design services; or 
0.6pp if we also include positive and negative spillovers such as wealth effects and 
limited crowding out of other investment.  One implication of these calculations is that 
growth in the rest of the economy—GDP growth minus the 0.2pp measured impact—is 
not as weak as often claimed.

### Page 7

Housing is likely to remain the weakest part of the economy in 2027 as very poor 
affordability continues to depress demand.  Single-family homebuilding has remained 
close to pre-pandemic levels so far even though mortgage rates are now 3.5pp higher.  
The main reason has been the housing shortage, which has meant that despite worse 
affordability, demand has remained sufficient to absorb the limited supply available 
without a large decline in home prices, which has sustained the incentive to build.  But 
with the housing shortage now less acute and margins for homebuilders normalizing, 
homebuilding is likely to become more sensitive to changes in interest rates.  Higher 
interest rates will also continue to dampen housing turnover by locking in homeowners 
who have low interest rates on their mortgages.

### Page 8

The Labor Market Outlook: A Slightly Softer Form of Full Employment 
While 2026 began with fears of a looming AI-driven layoff wave, jobless claims instead 
fell even further and the layoff rate remained historically low.  Coupled with a slow pace 
of hiring but a stabilization in job openings at a healthy level, this has left the labor 
market in a slightly softer low-hire, low-fire form of full employment.

Our slack tracker—a composite of ten measures of labor market tightness—likewise 
signals that the labor market is a touch softer than the unemployment rate alone 
suggests.  We expect the current equilibrium to persist in 2027, with job growth 
remaining healthy as the economy grows near potential. 
But companies will eventually want to use AI to reduce labor costs more meaningfully, 
and we expect faster displacement of workers from AI-exposed jobs to gradually push 
the unemployment rate higher in subsequent years.  The early evidence on how 
disruptive this will be is mixed.  The labor market already looks tougher for workers 
displaced from AI-exposed jobs, many of whom are moving into roles with similar or 
higher AI exposure that leave them vulnerable.  But young people, who have tended to 
adapt better historically, are already adjusting by shifting to college majors less exposed 
to AI displacement.

Historically Low; Job Openings Have Stabilized at a Healthy Level, Though Hiring Remains Soft

### Page 9

The convergence of our wage growth tracker and survey-based wage growth 
expectations suggests that wage growth is stabilizing at about 3.5%.  With productivity 
growth running above 2% this cycle, this implies subdued inflationary pressure from 
labor costs.  We see little reason to expect wage growth to accelerate meaningfully next 
year because the labor market is not unusually tight and workers appear to be aware 
that finding a new job is harder than usual and concerned about displacement risk from 
AI.

### Page 10

Shock, and AI Price Pressures 
The bulk of the upside surprise to inflation in 2026 came from the Iran war energy shock 
and AI price pressures, and the effects of those two forces and tariffs remain central to 
our inflation forecast for 2027.  The economy is not overheated at a macroeconomic 
level, and our bottlenecks tracker suggests it is not overheated at an industry level either 
outside of pockets tied to the AI boom.  Nor does high inflation appear to have become 
entrenched in expectations.  As a result, inflation should moderate as long as the effects 
of tariffs, the war, and AI price pressures diminish and no new shocks emerge.

The impact of earlier tariff rounds on consumer prices appears to be over, and the 
impact on year-over-year inflation should continue to shrink as the base effects from 
last year drop out.  We expect the White House to replace some of the tariffs that were 
ruled unconstitutional by the Supreme Court, but this should have only a modest impact 
because the tariff increase will likely be small and our analysis indicates that removing 
tariffs had a minimal effect on consumer prices, so restoring them should too.

### Page 11

The outlook for the Iran war remains uncertain, but our commodity strategists expect 
Brent oil prices to remain near $100/bbl over the next few months before easing 
gradually through 2027 to $80/bbl.  In that scenario, we estimate that two-thirds of the 
cumulative passthrough from higher commodity prices to core PCE prices is behind us, 
and that the impact on year-over-year core PCE inflation will rise from 0.5pp now to a 
peak of 0.6pp in coming months before declining to 0.1pp by the end of 2027.

The near-term outlook for AI price pressures is also quite uncertain, but our equity 
analysts expect memory price growth to slow sharply in 2027 as new production 
capacity boosts supply, and this should also restrain inflation for other consumer

and shelter, which is still running above the alternative data leading indicators of rent

The Fed Outlook: Two More Hikes in December and March, Followed by 
Cuts Starting in November 
Under our economic forecast, the FOMC would remain firmly focused on the inflation 
side of its mandate in 2027.  We expect two more 25bp rate hikes in December 2026 
and March 2027 for a total of three to a peak of 4.25-4.5%, though a third hike could 
come earlier in January or the FOMC could deliver more than three hikes if the next two 
inflation reports do not look better, as we expect.  We expect the FOMC to begin a series 
of gradual cuts back to 3.25-3.5% starting at the final meeting of 2027 in November (the 
meeting was previously scheduled for December) once inflation has declined.  Both our 
baseline and probability-weighted Fed forecasts are less hawkish than market pricing.

### Page 14

The FOMC will also consider the conclusions of the task forces next year.  We expect any 
changes to be limited.  The balance sheet task force is unlikely to lead to a large 
reduction in the size of the Fed’s balance sheet, though a shift in the composition of its 
assets toward bills is possible.  The communications task force could lead to changes to 
the Summary of Economic Projections, but we would be surprised if it were eliminated 
entirely because this would seem like a large step away from transparency.  The inflation 
frameworks task force could engage with a range of questions, from how to respond to 
inflation caused by supply shocks to what the best measure of inflation is for the Fed.  
We do not expect major policy changes to come out of the alternative data or 
productivity and jobs task forces. 
Risks to Our Forecast of Sturdy Growth and Lower Inflation in 2027 
The most obvious risk to our forecast of sturdy growth and a long-awaited decline in 
inflation is that the inflation shocks of the last two years, which we and most forecasters 
did not anticipate, could last longer, or new shocks could arise.  But our best guess is that 
there will be fewer big policy-driven macroeconomic shocks in 2027, and it would be a 
fairly high bar for, say, new tariffs or a reescalation of the war with Iran to have as large 
of an impact in rate of change terms as they did over the last two years. 
We also see several downside risks to growth.  Further, increases in oil prices would 
weigh on real income growth and consumption.  A longer period of higher interest rates 
could also weigh on growth and reignite fiscal concerns, though our strategists’ forecasts 
imply a partial reversal of the recent tightening in financial conditions in 2027.  Job 
displacement from AI could come earlier and faster than we expect.  And AI could also 
introduce new risks, such as a proliferation of cyberattacks enabled by advanced AI 
tools.  But most of these risks seem more likely to have moderate rather than 
recessionary effects.

## Document 461: 【國泰證期研究部】衛星產業-星間鏈路ISL為邁向太空AI重要關鍵技術-20261119

Source PDF: all report pdf/【國泰證期研究部】衛星產業-星間鏈路ISL為邁向太空AI重要關鍵技術-20261119.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1 
產業更新 – 衛星產業 
星間鏈路 ISL 為邁向太空 AI 重要關鍵技術 
What’s New –  星間鏈路逐步成熟，帶動衛星傳輸效率提升 
隨著低軌衛星產業由早期單純提供地面通訊覆蓋，逐步朝向全球高速寬頻、即時資料傳輸及太
空運算網路發展，星間鏈路（Inter-Satellite Link, ISL）已從衛星系統中的輔助通訊技術，逐
漸演變為支撐大型衛星星座運作的核心基礎設施。透過在衛星之間建立直接通訊鏈路，使資料
得以在軌道上進行多節點轉送，再由適當的衛星回傳至地面，進而降低對密集地面站的依賴，
並提升網路覆蓋範圍、路由彈性及系統備援能力。相較傳統射頻通訊，雷射通訊具備高頻寬、
窄波束及較低跨鏈路干擾等優勢，成為新一代衛星星座的重要發展方向。 
Trends/Outlook – SpaceX 持續提升 ISL 規模 
Starlink 已建立全球規模的太空雷射網狀通訊架構，使衛星接收的資料能夠透過多顆衛星進行
跨區域轉送，降低對地面閘道站的依賴，並有效擴大海洋、極地及偏遠地區的通訊覆蓋能力。
就產品規格而言，目前 Starlink既有衛星 V2 mini採用每顆配置 3組光學雷射終端的架構，單
鏈路最高傳輸速率達 200Gbps，而新一代 V3衛星則進一步增加至 6組雷射終端，單鏈路傳輸
速率提升至 400Gbps，顯示 SpaceX正同步透過增加終端數量及提高傳輸頻寬，擴充整體星座
的資料交換能力。此外，SpaceX 亦逐步將原本供內部使用的雷射通訊技術推向外部商業市場，
推出傳輸速率達 25Gbps的 Mini Laser終端，使第三方衛星得以接入 Starlink網路，提供在軌
資料中繼及高速資料回傳服務。 
Investment Summary 
1. 展望未來，ISL 將成為推動衛星產業由通訊服務邁向太空資訊基礎設施的重要關鍵技術。
預期 SpaceX Starmind中的 AI1運算衛星在獨立衛星 AI節點進行計算後，其結果將透過
ISL雷射互傳形成 AI 叢集，方便將上百上千顆的衛星結果進行聯通，並且及時回傳於地面。
故整體太空 AI資料中心的運行將大幅仰賴 ISL技術的成熟，看好相關衛星通訊元件廠商如
萊德光電(7717 TT)、昇達科(3491 TT)後市發展。 
2. 預期 SpaceX 26/27年新增 2,800/4,000 顆衛星，其中 27年除通訊衛星 V3外，新一代直
連服務衛星亦將起量。SpaceX甫順利完成第 15次星艦試射，將加速 V3衛星部屬，故持
續看好天上衛星 CCL與 PCB商台光電(2383 TT)、華通(2313 TT)後市發展。

2 
光學星間鏈路逐步成熟，帶動衛星傳輸效率提升 
隨著低軌衛星（LEO）產業由早期單純提供地面通訊覆蓋，逐步朝向全球高速寬頻、即時資料傳輸
及太空運算網路發展，星間鏈路（Inter-Satellite Link, ISL）已從衛星系統中的輔助通訊技術，逐
漸演變為支撐大型衛星星座運作的核心基礎設施。過去傳統衛星通訊主要仰賴衛星與地面閘道站之
間建立連線，衛星接收的資料需先回傳至地面，再透過地面骨幹網路進行交換與轉送，因此服務範
圍容易受到地面站部署位置、地理環境及基礎建設覆蓋程度限制。隨著 Starlink等大型低軌衛星星
座加速部署，衛星數量持續增加，全球通訊需求亦逐步延伸至海洋、極地、航空及其他地面網路難
以覆蓋的區域，使傳統高度依賴地面站的傳輸架構逐漸面臨限制，遞訊號路徑如同光透過平面鏡反
射一樣稱為彎管連結，衛星必須同時能被終端設備與地面站接收到，造成偏遠地區、海洋上若無地
面站建置則無法達成衛星通訊服務。 
圖一：衛星間傳輸路徑比較                    
資料來源：前瞻科技管理、國泰證期研究部整理 
尤其隨著光學星間鏈路（Optical ISL）逐漸成熟，透過在衛星之間建立直接通訊鏈路，使資料得以
在軌道上進行多節點轉送，再由適當的衛星回傳至地面，進而降低對密集地面站的依賴，並提升網
路覆蓋範圍、路由彈性及系統備援能力。相較傳統射頻通訊，雷射通訊具備高頻寬、窄波束及較低
跨鏈路干擾等優勢，成為新一代衛星星座的重要發展方向。 
表一：衛星雷射通訊與傳統 RF 通訊對照 
比較項目 衛星雷射通訊（Lasercom） 傳統微波（RF）通訊 
技術原理 利用近紅外光傳輸數位訊號 利用無線電波（如 Ka/Ku 頻段）傳輸 
傳輸頻寬／速率 極高 中低 
頻譜限制 無限制 受限，需嚴格申請頻道，資源擁擠 
安全性 高 低 
固定距離通訊成本 低 高 
設備／架設成本 初期極高（高精密對準系統） 成熟且低（技術標準化、地面設備普及） 
主要缺點 易受氣候影響，對準精度要求極高 頻譜稀缺、延遲較高、易受電子干擾 
資料來源：國泰證期研究部整理

新世代衛星、火箭升級短期影響衛星部屬速度 
SpaceX 為目前全球最積極部署 ISL 的衛星營運商之一，透過旗下 Starlink 低軌衛星星座，已將光
學星間通訊技術由早期研發驗證推進至大規模商業應用。SpaceX 最早於 2020 年開始進行衛星間
雷射通訊測試，並成功透過兩顆衛星傳輸大量數據；2021年 1月首次將搭載雷射星間鏈路的 10顆
Starlink衛星部署至極地軌道，隨後於同年 9月正式導入配備 ISL的 Starlink V1.5衛星，逐步將雷
射通訊技術擴展至大規模星座部署。  
隨著衛星數量持續增加，Starlink 已建立全球規模的太空雷射網狀通訊架構，使衛星接收的資料能
夠透過多顆衛星進行跨區域轉送，降低對地面閘道站的依賴，並有效擴大海洋、極地及偏遠地區的
通訊覆蓋能力。就產品規格而言，目前 Starlink既有衛星 V2 mini採用每顆配置 3組光學雷射終端
的架構，單鏈路最高傳輸速率達 200Gbps，而新一代 V3衛星則進一步增加至 6組雷射終端，單鏈
路傳輸速率提升至 400Gbps，顯示 SpaceX 正同步透過增加終端數量及提高傳輸頻寬，擴充整體
星座的資料交換能力。此外，SpaceX 亦逐步將原本供內部使用的雷射通訊技術推向外部商業市場，
推出傳輸速率達 25Gbps的 Mini Laser終端，使第三方衛星得以接入 Starlink網路，提供在軌資料
中繼及高速資料回傳服務。 
圖三：Starlink 雷射通訊系統示意圖 
資料來源：SpaceX、國泰證期研究部整理 
展望未來，隨著衛星直連終端、太空資料中心及軌道運算等應用逐步發展，ISL 的重要性將不再侷
限於改善地面網路覆蓋，而是進一步支援跨衛星資料交換、分散式運算及天地整合網路架構，成為
推動衛星產業由通訊服務邁向太空資訊基礎設施的重要關鍵技術。預期 SpaceX Starmind中的 AI1
運算衛星在獨立衛星 AI節點進行計算後，其結果再透過 ISL雷射互傳形成 AI 叢集，方便將上百上
千顆的衛星結果進行聯通，並且及時回傳於地面，故未來太空 AI資料中心的運行將大幅仰賴 ISL技
術的成熟。

マレーシア中銀は８会合連続で金利据え置き、先行きは利上げ転換も視野に 
～AI・半導体需要が景気を支えるなか、2027 年の物価上昇圧力の強まりが政策判断のカギに～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ マレーシア中銀は、11 月５日に開催した定例会合において、政策金利を８会合連続で 2.75％
に据え置くことを決定した。同国経済は、世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の拡大を追い風に
堅調に推移し、７-９月期の実質ＧＤＰ成長率（速報値）は前年同期比＋5.3％となった。中
銀は2026 年の成長率が５％を上回るなど、景気の先行きに強気の見方を示している。 
○ 一方、９月のインフレ率は同＋1.8％と落ち着いた推移が続いているものの、2027 年に向け
て物価上昇圧力が強まる可能性を指摘している。また、通貨リンギは輸出拡大による貿易黒
字の拡大などを背景に底堅く推移している。とはいえ、先行きも米国の金融政策や国際金融
市場の動向といった外部環境に左右されやすい転嫁が続くと見込まれる。 
○ 中銀は当面、金利据え置きによる様子見姿勢を維持すると予想される。さらに、景気の堅調
さが賃金や物価の持続的な上昇につながる場合には、2027 年前半にも利上げに転じる可能性
もある。ただし、世界経済の減速や地政学リスクなどの不確実性も残るため、今後の金融政
策は景気・物価動向や為替相場を慎重に見極めながら運営されると考えられる。

【マレーシア中銀は８会合連続で金利据え置き、景気と物価を慎重に見極める姿勢を維持】

５日に開催した定例の金融政策委員会
（ＭＰＣ）において、政策金利である翌

置くことを決定した（図１）。同行は、2025
年７月の定例会合において、米国の関税
政策などを巡る不確実性の高まりを背景
に、25bp の利下げに踏み切った。その後
は金利を据え置く様子見姿勢に転じてお
り、今回の金利据え置きは８会合連続と

### Page 2

2/4 
Ｉ（人工知能）・半導体関連需要の拡大を追い風に輸出の堅調な拡大が続き、国内の雇用・所得環境
も底堅く推移している。一方、インフレ率は落ち着いた推移が続いており、中銀は景気の底堅さと
物価の安定を踏まえ、現行の金融政策を維持することが適切と判断したと考えられる。 
会合後に公表された声明文では、世界経済について「技術関連投資の拡大や安定した雇用環境を
背景に底堅さが続いている」との認識を示した。一方で、「エネルギー価格の上昇に伴うコスト増加
が消費者物価に波及し、世界的なインフレ圧力は根強く残る」と指摘した。また、「地政学リスクの
長期化に加え、各国の金融政策調整に伴う金融環境の引き締まり、金融市場における資産価格の割
高感、財政面の脆弱性などが世界経済の下振れリスクになる」とした。ただし、「技術関連投資の一
段の拡大やサプライチェーンの回復が、景気を押し上げる可能性」にも言及した。こうしたなか、
中銀は現行の金利水準について「物価の安定と持続可能な経済成長の見通しに整合的」との評価を
維持しており、今後も景気と物価を巡るリスクのバランスを慎重に見極める姿勢を示した。

【ＡＩ・半導体需要の拡大を追い風に景気は堅調、2026 年の成長率は５％を上回る見通し】 
足元のマレーシア経済は、世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の拡大を追い風に、堅調な景気拡大が
続いている。７-９月期の実質ＧＤＰ成長
率（速報値）は前年同期比＋5.3％と前期
（同＋6.0％）から鈍化したものの、高い
成長率を維持していることが確認された
（図２）。前期比年率ベースでは＋6.21％
と試算されるなど、足元の景気は堅調に
推移している様子がうかがえる。産業別
では、サービス業で底堅い動きが続いた
ほか、製造業では伸びが加速しており、
なかでも電気・電子部品関連を中心とす
る輸出志向型産業の拡大が製造業の成長
をけん引する動きがみられた。また、建設業も堅調に推移しており、設備投資やデータセンター関
連投資の拡大も景気を下支えしている。 
外需についても、９月の輸出額は前年同月比＋54.5％と大幅に拡大した。世界的なＡＩ・半導体
関連投資の拡大を受けた同関連の旺盛な需要に加え、石油製品や機械・設備、ＬＮＧなどの輸出も
押し上げに寄与している。こうした外需の堅調さに加えて、国内においても、安定した雇用環境や
継続的な投資活動が内需を支えている。中銀は今回の声明文で、「2026 年の経済成長率が５％を上
回る」との見通しを示し、前回９月会合時点の見通し（５％程度）から上方修正した。さらに、2027
年についても、「技術関連需要の拡大や観光収入の増加に加え、2027 年度予算に盛り込まれた家計
支援策などが個人消費を下支えする」との見方を示した。一方で、イラン情勢の悪化が長期化した
場合の影響や資源生産の減少などには、引き続き警戒が必要である。

### Page 3

3/4 
【インフレ率は落ち着いた推移が続くも、2027 年には物価上昇圧力が強まる可能性を指摘】 
前述のように堅調な景気が続いているにもかかわらず、インフレ率は落ち着いた推移をみせてい
る。９月のインフレ率は前年同月比＋1.8％となり、前月（同＋1.9％）から伸びが鈍化した（図３）。
住居・水道・電気・ガス・その他燃料関連の伸びが鈍化したほか、食品・飲料関連の物価上昇圧力
も限定的となっている。さらに、変動の大きい品目などを除いたコアインフレ率は同＋1.7％と前
月から横ばいで推移しており、幅広い品目に物価上昇圧力が広がる動きとはなっていない。 
ただし、先行きの物価動向を巡っては
不透明感が残る。イラン情勢の悪化によ
る原油をはじめとする国際商品市況の
高止まりは、輸送費や製造コストの上昇
を通じて国内物価を押し上げる要因と
なる。また、異常気象による食料品供給
への影響に加え、旺盛な内需が企業によ
るコスト転嫁を容易にする可能性にも
注意が必要である。今回の声明文では、
「2027 年に向けて総合・コアの双方で
インフレ率が上昇する」と予想する一
方、「政府による政策対応や、安定した需要環境などを理由に上昇は抑制されたものにとどまる」と
の見方を示した。このため、足元では早急な金融引き締めを必要とする状況には至っていないもの
の、物価上昇圧力の持続性が今後の政策判断を左右することになると考えられる。

【リンギ相場は底堅い動きをみせるも、引き続き米国の金融政策や金融市場の動向に左右される】 
このところの金融市場においては、通
貨リンギの対米ドル相場は比較的底堅
く推移している（図４）。背景には、世界
的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の拡大を受け
た輸出の好調を受けて、貿易黒字が拡大
するなど、実需面からリンギが買われや
すい環境が続いていることがある。一
方、国際金融市場における投資家のリス
ク選好の変化や米ドル相場の動向も、リ
ンギ相場を左右する要因となっている
ことにも注意が必要である。 
このため、先行きのリンギ相場には上下双方のリスクが存在する。マレーシア経済の堅調な拡大
や輸出・対外収支の改善はリンギ相場の下支え要因となる一方、ＦＲＢ（米連邦準備制度理事会）
による金融引き締めが長期化すれば、米国との金利差がリンギ相場の上値を抑える可能性がある。

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
4/4 
また、イラン情勢が再び悪化するなど国際金融市場でリスク回避姿勢が強まれば、多くの新興国通
貨と同様にリンギにも売り圧力がかかることが懸念される。さらに、世界的な半導体需要が一服す
る場合には、輸出の伸びの鈍化を通じてリンギ相場の下押し要因となる可能性もある。こうしたな
か、中銀が今後、金融引き締め方向への政策転換を示唆するかどうかは、市場の金利見通しを通じ
てリンギ相場に影響すると予想される。

【2027 年は金融引き締めへの転換も視野に入るも、追加引き締めには物価上昇の持続性がカギ】 
先行きの金融政策を巡っては、当面は現行の金利水準を維持しつつ、2027 年には金融引き締めが
選択肢に浮上する可能性が考えられる。足元の同国経済は、外需の堅調さや内需の底堅さを背景に
５％程度の成長を維持しており、金融緩和を一段と強化する必要性は小さいと見込まれる。一方、
足元のインフレ率は比較的落ち着いているものの、中銀は 2027 年にかけて物価上昇圧力が強まる
との見方を示している。こうした状況を勘案すれば、将来的に金融引き締めへの転換を検討する余
地はある。ただし、今回の声明文は利上げの実施時期を示唆したものではなく、金融政策の方向性
が明確に転換したと判断するのは早計であろう。 
今後の政策運営は、外需主導による景気拡大の動きが、国内の賃金や物価上昇圧力へと波及する
かどうかがカギを握る。世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の拡大が続くとともに、内需も堅調に推移
し、インフレ率が持続的に上昇する場合には、中銀が 2027 年前半にも 25bp 程度の利上げに踏み切
る可能性がある。一方、イラン情勢の緊迫化による世界経済の減速や、米国の金融引き締めの影響
などを受けて輸出や設備投資が鈍化する場合には、金利据え置きによる様子見姿勢が一段と長期化
することも考えられる。このため、先行きの金融政策は、景気動向のみならず、輸入物価や企業に
よるコスト転嫁の広がり、さらにはリンギ相場を含む金融市場の動向を総合的に勘案しつつ、慎重
に運営されると予想される。

## Document 553: 内外経済ウォッチ『アジア・新興国～台湾､AI･半導体特需で高成長も､金融市場に懸念材料～』(2026年12月号)

Source PDF: all report pdf/内外経済ウォッチ『アジア・新興国～台湾､AI･半導体特需で高成長も､金融市場に懸念材料～』(2026年12月号).pdf
